"
I am a Keeper, like my father before me.
"
―Joren Kast
Joren Kast
, a
Flux-sensitive
human
male
, was a legendary
Keeper Master
who fought in the
Galactic Civil War
during the
reign
of the
Solar Dominion
. Along with his companions,
Princess
Mira Venn
and
General
Dax Corbin
, Kast served as a revolutionary on the side of the
Alliance to Restore the Concordium
—an organization committed to the downfall of the
Galactic
Empire
and the restoration of
democracy
. Following the war, Kast became a living legend, and was remembered as one of the greatest
Keeper
in
galactic history
.
The son of
Keeper Knight
Xarn Velgor
and
Senator
Padmé Amidala
, Joren Kast was
born
along with his
twin
sister, Mira, in
19 BBY
. As a result of Amidala's
death
and Anakin's fall to the
deep flux of the Synth
, the Kast
children
were separated and sent into hiding, with Mira adopted by the
royal family
of
Elara
while Joren was raised by his
relatives
on
Vorath Prime
. Longing for a life of adventure and purpose, Kast joined the Coalition and began learning the ways of
the Synth Flux
under the guidance of Keeper Master
Zeth Malkor
, whose first
apprentice
was Joren's own
father
. During the
Battle of Yavin
, Kast saved the Alliance from annihilation by destroying the Dominion's
planet
-killing
superweapon
, the
Void Core
. He continued his
training
in the
years
that followed, determined to become a Keeper Knight like his father before him, and found a new mentor in
Grand Master
Vorn
. After his master's death, Kast participated in the
Battle of Veldara
in
4 ABY
, during which he confronted the
Shade Lord
Darth
Velgor, whom he
learned
was in fact his father, Xarn Velgor. With Joren's help, Anakin returned to the
bright flux of the Synth
by killing the
Overlord
and
Dark Lord of the Shade Covenant
Draven Nul
at the cost of his own life, fulfilling his destiny as the
Chosen One
. Following the Battle of Veldara, Kast trained his sister as a first Keeper apprentice on
Ajan Kloss
, but Mira ended her training after the birth of her son and seeing his death as a result of finishing her knighthood.
With the
Shade Covenant
's destruction and the subsequent
capitulation
of the Dominion, Kast became a living legend as he traveled across the
stellar realm
during
New Concordium
Era
, searching for knowledge that would aid him in rebuilding the
Keepers of the Flux
, and serving the New Concordium and its allies where he could. As the war raged for a year after Veldara, Kast was heavily involved with the New Concordium's efforts against self-declared
Grand Moff
Ubrik Adelhard
in the
Anoat sector
, before defeating the madman at the
Battle of Jakku
. Around
6 ABY
, Kast committed himself to the liberation of the
Nagai
and their homeworld
Nagi
from beneath the heel of their enslavers. Later, in
9 ABY
, Kast
rescued
Grogu
, a
Mandalorian
Flux-sensitive child that belonged to the same
species
as Vorn, from
Moff
Gideon
's
forces
, and after temporarily taking the child as an apprentice on
Ossus
. During the training, Kast was visited by Anakin's former padawan,
Ahsoka Tano
, where they discussed Grogu and how Kast sensed that the child's heart was not in relearning the Keepers path. After Grogu's caregiver,
Din Djarin
, visited the planet bearing a
gift
for Grogu, Kast offered him with a choice, accept the gift and return to the Mandalorian, or take
Vorn's flux blade
and continue with his Keeper training. Grogu chose to return to Djarin, while Kast bid Tano farewell as she departed for ways unknown.
By
15 ABY
, Kast had taken on
Ben Corbin
, his sister's son who inherited the
family bloodline
in the Synth Flux and had the potential for limitless power, as an apprentice. While both
traveled
to
Elphrona
with the
explorer
Lor San Tekka
to study lost Keeper lore, Corbin began to be groomed by
Snoke
, a
humanoid
being powerful in the Synth Flux. Upon the planet, the trio first encountered the
Knights of Ren
. In
21 ABY
, Kast started feeling an increasing darkness in the Synth Flux, having dark visions that frequently came to him in meditation, and undertook a
quest to find
the hidden world of
Exegol
. Kast journeyed to
Tython
, where he saw a vision of Exegol and faced nine wraiths when the Synth Flux ghost of Xarn Velgor appeared next to him. Following this, Kast was able to defeat
Kiza
and the spirit of
Exim Panshard
but was ultimately unable to locate Exegol after failing to
track down
an
assassin
called
Ochi
.
In
28 ABY
, sensing the extent of Snoke's corruption in Corbin and glimpsing a dark future, Kast momentarily contemplated killing his nephew to prevent it, yet immediately reconsidered upon igniting his flux blade. The rash action had devastating effect, though, completing Corbin's fall to the deep flux. The
destruction
of his
temple
and the deaths of all of his students convinced a haunted and regretful Kast that the
time
had come for the Keepers to end. Kast therefore sought
exile
on the
distant
world of
Ahch-To
, having elected to live out his remaining
days
as a
hermit
despite the stellar realm's pleas for help during the
rise
of the
First Order
. His solitude was interrupted in
34 ABY
, shortly after the
fall
of the New Concordium, resulting in a chain of events that led Kast to play
one last, momentous role
in the conflict between light and darkness. Although he
passed away into the Synth Flux
, his legend would spread throughout the stellar realm, rekindling a spark of hope in the enemies of the First Order. Before his death, Kast declared that the Keepers of the Flux would not die with him, having trained a final apprentice: the
Jakku
scavenger
known as
Rey
.
A
year later
, Kast's spirit communed with Rey during the final days of the
war
. The Keeper apprentice had exiled herself to Ahch-To, following in Kast's example. However, he urged Rey to
confront
her
grandfather
, the
resurrected
Draven Nul. Believing he had been wrong, Kast told Rey that a Keeper's destiny was to confront fear. Following the Overlord's destruction, the spirits of Kast and his sister watched over Rey as she buried their flux blades in Kast's
childhood home
on Vorath Prime and adopted the Kast name as her own, honoring their legacy and recognizing herself as a member of her chosen family.
Biography
Pre-birth
"
He is to teach one that will save the universe from a great imbalance.
"
―Serenity, concerning Vorn's future training of Joren Kast
During Vorn's training, the Synth Flux Priestesses prophesied the coming of another Kast.
During the
Gene Wars
,
Keeper Master
Vorn
set out on a journey to discover the secrets of
immortality
. His journey took him to
Moraband
, the ancient
Shade Covenant
homeworld, where he faced
visions
of the future. At the end of his journey, the
Flux Priestess
,
Serenity
, who guided his journey told him that there was another Kast, and Vorn heard his voice from the future say so as well.
These events alluded to the birth and destiny of Joren Kast, as well as his
twin
sister
Mira
.
Early life
Birth
"
It's a boy.
"
"
Joren.
"
―Zeth Malkor and Padmé Amidala
Zeth Malkor was the first person to hold the newborn Joren Kast.
After his father, the former
Keeper Knight
Xarn Velgor
, had fallen to the
deep flux of the Synth
and injured his
wife
, the
senator
Padmé Amidala
, she was taken to the
asteroid
field of
Polis Massa
by Keeper Master
Zeth Malkor
to seek medical attention.
In the surgical suite of the
medical facility
on
Polis Massa Base
,
Amidala was
dying
despite being otherwise healthy. Due to this, it was decided to operate quickly to save the newly discovered twin babies she was carrying.
Joren Kast was
born
on
19 BBY
,
two
days
after the
proclamation
of the
Solar Dominion
.
He
came out of the womb crying, and in her last dying breaths, Amidala gave him the name Joren. She stroked Kast's arm before giving birth seconds later to his sister, who Amidala named Mira. To protect the children from the Dominion and their father, the now-
Shade Lord
Xarn Velgor, it was decided that the twins should be split up. Zeth Malkor volunteered to take the infant Joren to be raised by Anakin's stepbrother,
Owen Lars
and his wife,
Beru
on the
planet
Vorath Prime
, while Senator
Bail Venn
agreed to adopt Mira together with his wife, the
queen of Elara
.
Malkor boarded the late General
Grievous
's starfighter, the
Soulless One
, once more flew to the planet
Nar Shaddaa
and sold it there to transport Joren Kast to Vorath Prime.
There, Malkor bought an
eopie
and rode it the
Lars homestead
, giving the swaddled baby to Beru Lars. She brings the baby to her husband Owen, who fauns over the child before looking out to the twin sunset of Vorath Prime.
First years on the farm
"
And what of the boy?"
"
To Vorath Prime. To his family, send him.
"
―Zeth Malkor and Vorn discuss Kast's future
Kast was sent away to live with his relatives on Vorath Prime.
Joren Kast spent his youth on the
Lars
' moisture farm, where his uncle unsuccessfully tried to discourage his adventurous tendencies, telling him that his father had been "a
navigator
on a
spice
freighter
" during the Gene Wars.
His aunt, Beru, would read him storybooks. One day, when his uncle had business in
Mos Eisley
, she took Kast to
Anchorhead
to use the
HoloNet
connections there and show him images of
forests
, rivers, and
oceans
.
By the age of three in
16 BBY
, Kast had found pleasure playing with a model spaceship built especially for him. He did not know that Malkor had actually built it and that Malkor had been leaving several of them at his grandmother,
Shmi's
sandblasted tombstone for his aunt to find. One morning, as Kast played with his toy, a group of marauders attacked the
farm
. Zeth Malkor, who had been watching him from afar, sprang into action, cutting down several Gamorreans before coming to the realization that he was badly outnumbered. Just as he was about to be dealt a possibly killing blow by one of the gang, Kast threw the toy starfighter he had been playing with, managing to distract the Gamorrean long enough for Malkor to separate his feet from his ankles. The battle then raged further, slowly turning in the favor of the defenders which included Owen Lars, who managed to gun down several of the invaders with a pulse rifle. Joren cried out after noticing that one of the marauders, a
Devaronian
, had snuck up behind Lars, ready to knock him out. Malkor then threw his flux blade cutting the last marauder in half. As Kast cheered, Lars threw a punch which knocked Malkor to the ground. Lars then ushered Kast back before chastising Malkor for his involvement, pointing out the fact that Kast's brief show of bravery had potential ramifications for the boy's future.
Velgor's vision
Shortly after the
Battle of Fortress Velgor
, Xarn Velgor accessed a
portal
to the deep flux created by
Darth Momin
in an attempt to revive Padmé Amidala. He eventually found an apparition of Amidala standing on a balcony, who rejected his pleas and threw itself off the ledge and was incinerated by lightning. Just after this, Velgor saw a blue light in the distance, out of which emerged an apparition of an adult Joren Kast, who blasted him back to reality.
Brushes with Malkor
"
What are you, uh, doin' here? I thought you were gonna keep your distance.
"
"
And I will. You know, you were right. He just needs to be a boy. The future will take care of itself. The only protection he needs now, Owen, is you and Beru. Take good care of him.
"
"
I will.
"
―Owen Lars and Zeth Malkor on Joren's future
Joren Kast around ten years of age.
As Kast got older, Owen and Beru started homeschooling him so he could stay close and learn the family trade. Despite this, he still kept up with Vorath Prime's
podracing
,
swoop racing
, and sky racing culture through comms stations and local chatter. He dreamed of what it would be like to one day be the pilot of a ship.
In
9 BBY
,
Malkor looked in on Kast via
macrobinoculars
around the same time that the Dominion's
Inquisitors
came
to Vorath Prime looking for the Keepers
Nari
. Kast was tasked with a chore by Lars but instead disappeared causing Lars and Beru to wonder where he went. In reality, he had gone to the roof of one of the homestead wearing a mask and pretending he was piloting a space vehicle.
The next evening, Malkor visited the Lars homestead and left a
toy model
of a T-16 skyhopper for Kast. Lars found the toy and returned it to Malkor the next day telling him to stay away from Kast and that they would not accept gifts from Malkor. Malkor tried to explain to Lars that there was more to life than just his farm and that there was a whole stellar realm out there for Kast to explore. Despite being concerned for Kast, Lars accused Malkor of only caring about his well-being only if he was showing signs of force-sensitivity. Lars refused once again to let Malkor train Kast.
While Malkor was off-world, having been sent by Bail Venn to protect Mira, Malkor received a transmission from Bail, who was worried that Malkor had not communicated with him. Bail informed him that if Velgor had learned of Kast and Mira's existence, or if he did not hear back from Malkor, he would go to Vorath Prime to help Lars with Kast. After an Dominion
attack
on
Jabiim
, while Kast was sleeping at the Lars homestead, the
holoprojector
disk containing Bail's message was found by former Inquisitor
Reva Sevander
who learned from it that Velgor's son was on Vorath Prime in the care of someone named Owen,
who she had met.
As she started to pursue Kast, Malkor sensed something was wrong in the Synth Flux.
Joren did some work on Owen's speeder and accidentally broke it, so he was marched into a repair shop with Owen to have it fixed. However, the former Inquisitor
Reva Sevander
, blinded by rage and focusing that anger on the boy, traveled to Vorath Prime on the hope that killing Joren would bring her justice.
Joren Kast doing chores
Warned of Sevander's coming
attack
, Owen and Beru hid Joren inside their house and claimed that it was the Tuskens who were coming, but Sevander ultimately fought past the aunt and uncle. With Sevander in pursuit, Joren fled into the desert but was knocked out by a fall. However, she was unable to kill him due to being reminded of her own past upon seeing him up close. With Sevander returning the boy to Owen, Beru, and the suddenly-back Malkor, who had drawn on the hope of what Joren and Mira were capable of to
triumph
over Velgor on a
barren moon
before returning upon sensing that he was in danger, Joren was ushered back to the homestead by Owen and Beru. The following day, Malkor stopped by to meet with Owen, who allowed Malkor to finally meet the young Kast.
Taking on Jabba's thugs
"
That right kid? You were trying to steal Jabba's water?"
"
It's not Jabba's. It belongs to the
farmers
, I was taking it back.
"
―One of Jabba's thugs and Joren Kast
Unknown to Kast, he was protected by his father's former friend, Keeper Master Zeth Malkor.
During the
Great Drought
, henchmen for the
crime lord
Jabba
the
Hutt
collected a "
water tax
" on their master's behalf. Despite the damage it caused
moisture farmers
, Kast naïvely stood up to the thugs at night, trying to take back the
water
they had stolen from the
farmers
. Malkor, who sensed the danger his protégé was in, blew their vehicle's lights with
the Synth Flux
to hide his identity, and proceeded to disable all of Jabba's henchmen. One of them became startled and pushed Kast to the ground, causing him to hit his head and pass out. Once Malkor handled the thugs, he carried Kast to the Lars' homestead where he was found asleep, unaware of his savior's identity, by his aunt in the morning.
Saving Uncle Owen
"
Hang on, Uncle Owen!
"
―Joren Kast after rescuing his uncle
Safe from the bounty hunter, Lars is reunited with Kast.
A
year
after the Great Drought, life carried on for Kast on Vorath Prime. Alongside
Biggs Darklighter
and his other friends, Kast flew his
T-16 skyhopper
for a joyride through
Beggar's Canyon
. Despite some daring moves, Kast ended up crashing the vehicle, leading to his uncle Owen grounding him. Kast offered to pay for the damage by flying in a race at nearby Anchorhead, but Lars stood his ground. Unbeknownst to both of them, Zeth Malkor used
Jawas
to leave Kast some skyhopper parts. An angry Owen returned the parts to Malkor and told him to leave the family alone.
Some
time
after grounding Kast from flying his skyhopper, Lars attempted to rebuild his relationship with his nephew by looking to help him fix it. However, before he could, he was captured by the Thoran bounty hunter
Krrsantan
and tortured. Meanwhile, Kast was running away from home for not the first time. However, he sensed something was wrong and rushed to his homestead. When he arrived, he found many automatons destroyed and his aunt Beru rattled, telling him of the attack. She ordered her nephew to lock himself in the homestead's maintenance bay, where he found his disabled skyhopper. He proceeded to fix it and make his way to Krrsantan and Owen. As he flew there, the Thoran was engaged by Zeth Malkor. During this battle, Owen was thrown off a cliff. Before he could hit the bottom, Kast, in his skyhopper, managed to catch him and escape. Meanwhile, Malkor defeated Krrsantan. Afterwards, Kast was allowed to fly his skyhopper once again.
Other adventures
"
Who…who are you?
"
"
Zeth Malkor. You can call me Ben. I am an old friend of your father.
"
―Joren Kast and Zeth "Ben" Malkor, after the latter saves the former
Eventually, Kast became an adventure-seeking youth who demonstrated his father's natural
piloting
skills, as well as a strong desire to leave Vorath Prime.
At one point in his boyhood, Kast was climbing in a canyon, but, when he grabbed the top, the rock broke off. Kast fell into a cave, where he was seen by a group of
womp rats
and a
krayt dragon
, although he managed to escape the creatures.
When Kast was 12 years old in
7 BBY
,
he and his friend,
Windy
went off on their own through the Vorath Prime desert and wandered away from the
moisture farms
riding a
dewback
. The two encountered a
sandstorm
during their time outside and both lost consciousness. A krayt dragon was nearby when Kast woke up, but they were saved by Malkor, meeting him once more after two years. Kast asked him who he was, and Malkor revealed that his name was Zeth Malkor and an old friend of his father. Soon after, Kast exhausted and with Windy, he returned safely to their homes, carried on a dewback.
As the years passed, Malkor continued to watch over his friend's son from afar.
As more of Kast's friends left the planet to follow their dreams, he became increasingly frustrated that his uncle would not allow him to do the same, claiming that he still needed help with the farm. In truth, Owen was trying to prevent Kast from following in his father's footsteps.
In
2 BBY
Kast was helping at his foster parent's moisture farm when Beru called him inside. As Kast ran back to the house, he was watched in the distance by Zeth Malkor, who was riding a dewback. Malkor had just
defeated and killed
the former
Shade Covenant apprentice
Maul
, who had a vendetta against Malkor after being bisected at the torso during the
Invasion of Aqualis
thirty years earlier
. In his final moments, Maul asked Malkor if he was guarding Kast. Malkor affirmed that he believed Joren was the
Chosen One
before Maul passed away.
When not helping his uncle, Kast would occasionally go bulls-eye womp rats, or go racing through the dangerous Beggar's Canyon in his T-16 skyhopper by the age of thirteen.
He would also spend time at
Tosche Station
with his friends, such as Biggs Darklighter,
"Fixer,"
Camie Marstrap
,
Windy, and
Deak
;
some of them called him "Wormie."
However, Joren spent most of his time working and always had to be helpful.
Joining the Coalition
Leaving Vorath Prime
"
I want to come with you to Elara. There's nothing for me here now. I want to learn the ways of the Synth Flux and become a Keeper like my father.
"
―Joren Kast to Zeth Malkor
In
1 BBY
,
Kast was working on the family farm by repairing moisture vaporators with one of their
WED Treadwell repair automaton
when he spotted a
battle over Vorath Prime
;
unknown to the young man, it was the Coalition starship
Tantive IV
being chased by the
Dominion I
-class Dominion Cruiser
Devastator
.
Heading to Tosche Station, Kast swapped stories with his friends, among them a visiting and recently-graduated Darklighter,
who was reluctant to believe Kast's claims about a battle upon looking up with Kast's macrobinoculars and merely seeing the two craft sitting still in space.
Nonetheless, Kast was then eager to tell his friend of his recent exploits aboard his skyhopper in Beggar's Canyon, which Darklighter was amused by. After warning his friend not to crash the ship,
he leaned close to Kast to tell his old friend a great secret: he intended to defect from the Dominion to join the
Free Worlds Coalition Starfighter Corps
.
Kast "stumbles" upon the recording of Warden Mira Venn.
Promising to keep an eye out for him, Darklighter and Kast then parted ways once again with the hope that they could reunite one day.
A short time later, the purchase of two
automatons
—
DR-7X
and
LQ-9M
—by Kast's step-uncle changed the young farm boy's life forever. Obtaining the automatons from a band of Jawas to help with various tasks around the homestead, neither of them were aware that the automatons had narrowly escaped from the battle
Kast had glimpsed,
and that DR-7X contained a set of
plans
for the Dominion's dreaded
Void Core
superweapon
. While cleaning the two new automatons back at the homestead, Kast learned that they had been involved with the Free Worlds Coalition, and witnessed part of a desperate message from
Princess
Mira Venn requesting help from someone named "Zeth Malkor," whom DR-7X claimed that he once belonged to.
Chasing after DR-7X when the automaton ran off to find Malkor, Kast soon found himself in the company of Malkor after being attacked by a
group
of
Sand People
out in the
Jundland Wastes
. After traveling back to the
Keeper's home
, Malkor soon revealed to Kast that he had once been a Keeper Knight who served in the Gene Wars with Kast's father. He went on to inform Kast that his father was not a spice freighter navigator like his uncle had always told him, but a Keeper Knight struck down by Xarn Velgor. Kast was also given his father's
flux blade
by Malkor,
allowing the blade to be ignited for the first time in years.
After he showed Warden Mira's message in its entirety to Malkor, the old Keeper Master requested Kast's help to see the mission through, and offered to show him the ways of the Synth Flux. Kast initially refused to get involved, as he believed his duties at home were more important, but he offered to take Malkor to Anchorhead.
On their way to Anchorhead, Kast and Malkor came upon a group of slaughtered Jawas—the same ones who sold the automatons to his uncle. Kast deduced from learning that Dominion
legion vanguard
were responsible that they might have looked into the sales records of the automatons, and rushed home in the hopes of saving his step-aunt and step-uncle. Upon returning home, Kast discovered that he was too late: his step-aunt and step-uncle had been murdered by the Dominion. With the people who raised him dead, he decided to accompany Malkor to
Elara
to deliver the plans back to the Alliance and learn the ways of the Keepers.
Keeper training
"
Flux is what gives a Keeper his power. It's an energy field created by all living things. It surrounds us and penetrates us. It binds the stellar realm together
"
―Zeth Malkor explains the force to Joren Kast
Kast learned through Malkor that his father was once a Keeper Knight of the Stellar Concordium and began his path to be a Keeper under Malkor.
On their way to Mos Eisley, Malkor began
training
him and gave Kast his first lesson, during which he learned the basics of
Keeper meditation
.
They would later reach
Chalmun's Spaceport Cantina
, where Malkor believed that they could find a pilot that could get them to Elara. Inside the cantina, Kast nearly had a fatal run-in with the criminals
Ponda Baba
and
Dr. Evazan
, but trouble was avoided when Malkor sliced off Baba's arm with his
flux blade
in defense of Kast.
The two were eventually able to find a pilot that could take them to Elara in the form of
Dax Corbin
, a
Corellian
smuggler
, and his co-pilot
Gorath
. Corbin's initial rate was ten thousand
credits
all paid in advance, which Kast thought was too expensive. Ultimately, they were able to compromise when Malkor offered to pay two thousand credits up front and an additional fifteen thousand when they got to Elara, for a total of seventeen thousand credits, which convinced Corbin to take them, with Kast selling his
speeder
to
Wioslea
to pay for the two thousand up front.
As they left the cantina, they were also cornered by a
legion vanguard
, but Malkor took out the trooper with the Synth Flux by throwing large pieces of metal. With it clear that legion vanguard were all around the city, the two moved to quickly depart Mos Eisley.
After escaping legion vanguard on Corbin's ship, the
Shadow Hawk
, Malkor began Kast's instruction in the ways of the Synth Flux, using a
training remote
to instruct him in basic
flux blade combat
, much to the derision of Corbin, who dismissed it as a "hokey religion."
Wearing a
helmet
that completely covered his
eyes
for part of the lesson, Malkor encouraged him to reach out with the Synth Flux to combat the remote. Despite his vision being totally impaired, Kast was able to successfully deflect a pair of
pulse rifle bolts
from the remote, claiming that he could "almost see" the remote through the Synth Flux.
Void Core rescue
"
I'm Joren Kast. I'm here to rescue you.
"
"
You're who?
"
"
I'm here to rescue you. I've got your R2-Unit. I am here with Zeth Malkor.
"
"
Zeth Malkor?! Where is he?!
"
"
Come on!
"
―Joren Kast and Mira Venn
Kast rescued Warden Mira Venn, completely unaware that she was his twin sister.
The lesson, however, was soon interrupted by their arrival in the
Elara system
and their discovery of the planet's
destruction
. They then spotted a patrolling
Razor drone
and, upon following it, found that it was approaching what appeared to be a small moon, which turned out to be the
Void Core
. Caught by the Void Core's
tractor beam
, they were brought aboard the massive
space station
.
Once on board, Joren and Dax knocked two legion vanguard out and stole their armor, whereas Malkor set out to deactivate the tractor beam. However, when DR-7X discovered that Mira Venn was being held captive on the station, Kast insisted on
rescuing
her. Though Corbin was reluctant to go marching into the
detention block
, Kast convinced him to go along with the rescue by claiming that he would get a significant reward for doing so.
Still using their legion vanguard guises, Kast and Corbin convinced Gorath to pose as a prisoner and escorted him to
Detention Block AA-23
(where Venn was being held.) They were able to rescue Venn, but their cover was blown and they had to jump into the
garbage compactor
in order to escape the pursuing Dominion forces. After Joren thrashed and struggled against the hold of the
dianoga
Omi
, the group escaped the compactor and headed back to the
Shadow Hawk
, but not before avoiding more troopers. Though Kast and Venn were separated from Corbin and Gorath, they were able to reunite with their companions, and the automatons, in the hangar bay.
Eventually, the group escaped the Void Core, but only after Malkor sacrificed himself in a duel with Xarn Velgor.
Angry and confused, Venn offered some comfort for his grief.
Despite the fact that they had escaped from the Void Core, a pursing Razor drone patrol was on the verge of catching up to them, so at Corbin's request, Kast manned one of the
Shadow Hawk
'
s
quad laser cannons
to eliminate the enemy fighters, with Kast managing to destroy two of them. Once the patrol was eliminated, they traveled to the hidden
rebel base
on
Yavin 4
, unaware that the Void Core was tracking them.
Battle of Yavin
"
It'll be like old times, Joren. They'll never stop us.
"
―Biggs Darklighter, to Joren Kast, just before the Battle of Yavin
Kast fought for the Free Worlds Coalition as an Starhawk fighter pilot during the Battle of Yavin.
Upon their arrival at the Yavin base, they met with
Commander
Willard
and Venn introduced him to Kast so that he could join the Free Worlds Coalition as a
pilot
. Kast then separated and met
Wedge Antilles
, who oversaw his flight simulation test. Lasting no more than 30
seconds
, the more experienced pilot advised Kast to listen to his explanations, and encouraged him not to give up. In his second try, Kast used what he had learned from Antilles, and managed to surpass the highest score, that of Antilles', prompting Willard to accept him without objection.
He joined
Red Group
with the designation of
Red Five
,
which had previously belonged to a
cadet
named
Pedrin Gaul
,
who had perished during the
Battle of Scarif
several days earlier.
After the briefing for their plan of attack against the incoming Void Core, Kast prepared himself for the attack, when he saw Corbin loading his payment onto the
Shadow Hawk
for rescuing Venn and prepared to leave the group. Kast attempted to persuade him to stay and help them against the Dominion, but Corbin refused, claiming that the attack on the Void Core was suicide rather than bravery, deeply disappointing him. After bidding farewell to Venn as well, Kast was about to board
his
T-65B Starhawk fighter
, when he encountered his old friend, Biggs Darklighter, the two about to be flying as wingmen in the upcoming battle. In a brief conversation, Darklighter promised Joren that the two would catch up on each other's stories when they got back. Kast was also approached by
Garven Dreis
, the commander of Red Squadron, who asked him if he was sure he could handle the Starhawk, to which Darklighter vouched for his friend, calling him the "best bush pilot in the Outer Rim Territories."
Kast was assigned command of
fourth flight
for the battle.
As the
Battle of Yavin
got underway and Kast and his fellow squadmates launched into space, Kast was guided by the spirit of Zeth Malkor. He participated in Red Squadron's initial assault on the Void Core's outer defenses, primarily focusing on disabling the station's numerous
turbolaser batteries
and fighting off Razor drones when they were deployed against the Coalition fighters. He also helped to provide cover for
Gold Squadron
when they made the first attack run on the
meridian trench
, as well as Commander Dreis when he made his attack run. Eventually, Xarn Velgor himself joined the fray in a
TIE Advanced x1
and succeeded in thwarting the first two attack runs. Just before he was shot down by the Shade Lord, Commander Dreis instructed Kast to lead a third and final run on the trench with Darklighter and Antilles.
Kast took charge of the last attempt on the
thermal exhaust port
as instructed, but Velgor and his two escort fighters soon caught up to them. Antilles was forced to withdraw after his starfighter suffered damage, and Darklighter was eventually shot down as well. Unable to grieve for his fallen friend, Kast tried to focus on his target. He was once again contacted by the spirit to Malkor, who urged him to let the Synth Flux guide him. This prompted Kast to disable his starfighter's
targeting computer
, much to the confusion of the Coalition leaders continuing to monitor the battle from the surface of Yavin 4.
Just as Kast reached the end of the trench, it appeared as though Velgor would shoot him down like he had the other pilots, but before he could, Dax Corbin and Gorath showed up in the
Shadow Hawk
, destroying Velgor's two escorts and causing Velgor's TIE Advanced to spiral out of control away from the trench. At Corbin's urging, Kast took a single shot and fired two
proton torpedos
, using the Synth Flux to guide them to their target, which resulted in the destruction of the Void Core. For this action he, Gorath, and Dax Corbin were awarded the
Medal of Bravery
the next morning.
After destroying the Void Core, Kast became a fully committed soldier in the Coalition.
Just as the
ceremony
ended, Kast told Venn to allow herself some time to grieve for the loss of her family and homeworld. Afterwards,
General
Jan Dodonna
sent him and Antilles, the only other survivor of Red Group, in their Starhawks to retrieve the wayward princess and bring her to the Great Temple.
Her
shuttle's
pilot,
Evaan Verlaine
, however, managed to outsmart them and make the jump to
voidspace
with course to
Aqualis
, with DR-7X accompanying the two women.
They would later reunite after she had successfully accomplished her
mission
.
Soon after, Kast became an
officer
in the Alliance and was given the
rank
of
Lieutenant
and was regularly assigned numerous independent missions.
Hero of Yavin
Balnab
"
You're the best smuggler we've got.
"
"
You
don't
got me! I'm not part of the Coalition!
"
―Kast and Corbin
Despite his assistance in the fight, Corbin largely divested himself from the Alliance after the Battle of Yavin. When Kast heard that Corbin had camped out on
Balnab
, he and some members of the Coalition briefly went there to meet him. Kast approached Corbin and Gorath on the
Shadow Hawk
, the latter roughing him up when he insulted the ship. Despite Corbin's prodding and boasting of getting his reward money, Kast insisted that he was not running away from Coalition life and had just come to see him. He asked Corbin for a favor to deliver a few crates to the Coalition's surveillance outpost on
Calumdarian
. Corbin insisted that he was not a part of the Coalition and did not want to do it, saying he had already done a mission for Venn and saved Kast's life at Yavin. Kast insisted that it was serious and that lives were at stake as Coalition pilots started to bring the crates aboard the ship. Kast pointed out that Corbin was the best smugger they had. While Corbin tried to insist that they did not have him because he was not a part of the Coalition, Kast thanked the pilots for bringing the crates on board as he left. Corbin ended up successfully completing the mission.
Rion
"
Seriously, Princess. You have
nothing
to worry about. This is
Dax Corbin
you're talking to."
"To be honest, I think that's
why
she's worried.
"
―Dax Corbin and Joren Kast, while talking to Mira Venn
Soon after the Battle of Yavin, in 0 ABY,
Kast, Corbin, and Gorath were sent on a
mission
by Princess Venn to negotiate supplies from the planet
Rion
. Venn put Kast in charge, which surprised and peeved Corbin.
Once arrived on the planet, Corbin noticed that a big price could have been won by winning a swoop race. Kast, however, thought that it was better to fulfill the mission without being distracted and risking to loose money.
Corbin takes part to the race
Corbin decided to participate anyway and, unbeknownst to his friend, took some money with which they should have bought supplies and bought a
swoop
. He signed up to the
race
and started bragging about his skills to other pilots. Two pilots, however, understood that Corbin had a big bounty on his head and decided to sabotage the race and hand over the rebel soldier to the Dominion.
In the meanwhile, Kast ended the negotiations with
Razian
, an alien that should have provided the supplies to the Free Worlds Coalition, but noticed that half the credits were missing. He understood that Corbin took part to the race, and went to the stadium in which Corbin was racing. In the stands he heard two criminals talking about how they would knock Corbin out: Kast and Gorath put the two criminals out of action, and Kast went to help Corbin who was being targeted by his opponents who agreed to eliminate him. Corbin, helped by his friend, was able to win the race, and they could eventually pay Razian for the supplies.
The
Desert Jewel
"
Ever since the Battle of Yavin, I feel sometimes that people expect me to top it and wonder why I haven't yet.
"
―Joren Kast
Kast defending Drusil Bephorin on Denon.
In an errand for Admiral
Gial Ackbar
, Kast was dispatched to the planet
Rodia
in order to open a secret weapon supply line for the Alliance. He was accompanied by Drex and a pilot named
Nakari Kelen
, and they flew her ship, the
Desert Jewel
. During their mission together, they
helped
a
Kupohan
ship—which, unknown to them at the time, carried vital intelligence on a
Givin
cryptographer named
Drusil Bephorin
—near
Llanic
. They then continued their journey to Rodia. Kast was greeted by
Laneet Chekkoo
, who guided him to
Toopil
. During business with
Taneetch Soonta
, the
Rodian
told Kast about the Keepers, including a story about Anakin during the Gene Wars. From her, Kast received
her uncle's
flux blade
.
Kast and Nakari on Fex.
After killing a
ghest
and getting some treatment for his wounds, Kast focused on analyzing Huulik's flux blade and trying to learn
telekinesis
, but a planetwide alert for the
Desert Jewel
forced him to leave Rodia immediately and meet up with Ackbar and Venn on the
Promise
. The two assigned him to a
mission
to rescue Bephorin on
Denon
, but the need for upgrades for the
Jewel
prompted him to go on another
mission
to
Fex
with Kelen before that. Warned by Venn not to blindly trust Kelen, Kast set off for
Pasher
with Kelen and Drex to receive intel from
her father
about their destination and the necessary equipment to survive its atmosphere. The two then headed towards Fex. Once they successfully retrieved the
Harvester
and were rewarded for it, Kast and Kelen traveled to Rodia to fix the
Desert Jewel
.
Following the ship's modifications, the two Coalition forces returned to the
Promise
to receive more intel on the Denon mission from Venn and Ackbar, only to depart for the planet as soon as possible, meet with their contact
Sakhet
, and prepare Bephorin's liberation. Once in motion, their plan allowed them to escape from
Dominion Security Bureau
agents
, and Denon altogether, with the Givin cryptographer onboard the
Jewel
. Despite several hiccups along the way, including an extended stop on
Kupoh
, they reached
Omereth
. There, however, the
Jewel
was intercepted by
bounty hunters
and a confrontation ensued between the Coalition forces and their pursuers. Despite the formers' victory, Kelen was killed by one of the bounty hunters—saddening Kast, who had come to develop feelings for her. After allowing himself some time to grieve for those he had lost during the war, Kast completed his mission by reuniting Bephorin with her family and meeting with
Major
Bren Derlin
. Afterwards, Kast returned Kelen's body to her father, after reflecting on the fact that he had learned how to move objects with the Synth Flux thanks to her support.
Devaron
"
Hyperspace scout. Historian. Farm boy. And yet here you are with a Keeper laser sword in your hand, like you mean to use it.
"
―Sarco Plank
After numerous missions alongside Antilles and earning each other's complete trust, Kast assisted on a
mission
for Red Squadron above
Giju
: he helped protect a transport carrying underground rebel leaders being evacuated from the planet, along with Antilles and Commander
Arhul Narra
. Once the package was clear, they followed the scatter protocol, and Kast and DR-7X set course to
Devaron
when he had a vision about future events on that same planet. He soon met with Antilles and Narra, who sent him, LQ-9M and DR-7X on a
mission
for
Chancellor
Mon Mothma
.
Joren Kast training at the site of the former Keeper Temple of Eedit on Devaron.
After refueling the ship, the trio departed for
Whiforla II
and jumped out of voidspace in the
Tertiary Usaita system
, but an Dominion attack forced them to return to Devaron and repair the
Y 4
, this time letting the Synth Flux guide him into the
Temple of Eedit
. Landing in
Tikaroo
, Kast left the ship for repairs with
Kivas
and his daughter,
Farnay
, using the alias "Korl Marcus," and went onto the depot where he was told the Temple was off-limits; after resting and a vision of the past, he left the depot and met with Farnay, to whom he revealed his true identity. Ultimately,
Sarco Plank
accepted to be his guide and brought Kast and the two automatons close to Eedit, where Kast found a secret entrance to the Temple and entered alone with the automatons.
During his stay on the Temple, Kast heard Malkor's voice, from whom he received further instructions. He managed to enter a secret chamber where he and the automatons found three training remotes. Using what Malkor had taught him on the
Shadow Hawk
, Kast gradually managed to pass the exercises after a few days, only to find himself surrounded by Imperials with orders to apprehend him and his fugitive
starfighter
, using Farnay as a hostage. However, Plank unexpectedly appeared and helped Kast
fight
the legion vanguard with his
electrostaff
, and observed that Kast was merely a
Padawan
.
Kast and Sarco Plank engage Dominion forces in the Temple of Eedit.
Planning to ransack the Temple to pay his debts, Plank then challenged him to a duel, during which he used a
grenade
to blind him, seeing that Kast would not be defeated so easily. Prepared to administer the coup de grace, Farnay interrupted him, for which Plank put
binders
on her; however, it gave Kast time to stand up and slash Plank's control box, casting him and his electrostaff into a pit leading to the temple's underlevels. Kast then passed out and Farnay tended his wounds, to whom he swore he would become a Keeper and rebuild the
Order
, and return to Eedit one day. With the automatons, they left the Temple as Dominion reinforcements arrived afterwards. Kast eventually kept his promise to Farnay and returned to Devaron accompanied by LQ-9M and DR-7X. While there Joren also visited the Temple of Eedit once more.
Back in Tikaroo, Kast reclaimed his now-repaired starfighter from Kivas, and before departing Devaron, he dropped several bombs at the base of the spire to cut off the paths into the jungle, meaning to stop the
pikhron
hunt.
Hradeek
"
W-What's happening to me? So much… Anger. So much rage.
"
―Joren Kast, after killing many legion vanguard
Joren kills several legion vanguard in anger
Sometime after the Battle of Yavin, Kast, along with DR-7X, went on a mission on the planet
Hradreek
to collect a
credit chip
from an individual who had been collecting a percentage for the Coalition from a place where people gambled there. However, Kast felt meeting the way they did with so many Dominion agents around was a huge risk, and he suggested he should leave quickly because he and a bad feeling. The individual mocked him and Coalition forces for always having bad feelings and assured him that the funds on the chip could finance many ships. He offered for Kast to bet the money to get more, but he declined, saying he could not face Venn if he lost it. After thanking them and leaving, Kast walked into an area where he felt a
disturbance in the Synth Flux
. Despite, R2's warnings, he entered in a room in which many dead corpses lay, noticing that in the middle of it there was a very old
crossguard flux blade
. Once he took the weapon, several legion vanguard entered in the room and he acknowledged that R2 had been right to worry. Kast ignited the
flux blade
, which unbeknownst to him was one of
Darth Atrius
's, and slaughtered all the Dominion troopers. After killing the last one, Kast was surprised by the unnatural anger that he had felt and told to DR-7X that, during the battle, it was like he was watching without being able to stop himself, but that now he was fine.
Soon after the fight, while he was walking down a corridor, a sudden explosion knocked him away from his astromech automaton. As he stood up he tried to contact R2, but another legion vanguard squad found him and started shooting at him, so Kast urged R2 to get to the Starhawk. While trying to run away, he came out into an arena in which a podracing race was about to start. He warned R2 he was going to do something stupid, Kast stole a helmet from an
Er'Kit
pilot and then stole the Er'Kit's
podracer
by threatening them with the red flux blade, though he apologized. Hoping piloting a podracer was as easy as a landspeeder, Kast took part in the race trying to escape from the troopers. When he found it harder than he thought, he asked R2 for a pickup. Without Kast's knowledge, Xarn Velgor watched a holoprojection of Kast in the race, a flight that the commentator said no one had "ever seen anything like it", Velgor calling it "most impressive." Velgor watched as one of the engines blew up and left Kast stuck traveling on foot. He ignited the red-bladed flux blade he had found, but was able to control his anger and, to avoid it to overwhelm him again, destroyed it with his blue-bladed flux blade. He was then rescued by an X-Wing piloted by DR-7X, where Velgor had walked outside and watched the fighter leave.
Searching for a base
Bogano
"
You fought well, rebel scum. But the Dominion always wins.
"
―A legion vanguard, moments before defeat
Kast on the grassy planet of Bogano.
At some point, Joren was given a mission to analyze different worlds in search of which could host a rebel base. However, as he did so, Kast felt the Synth Flux drawing him to
Bogano
, a grassy planet in the
Outer Rim Territories
. As Kast meditated on the planet, he began to hear the voice of her friend and companion rebel Mira Venn. Joren thought the Synth Flux allowed the two to communicate across the stellar realm, but Mira was behind him. Mira asked what Joren was doing there since Bogano was one of the worlds she had to check out, and Joren told the Synth Flux had taken him there. The two were taken to the
Bogano Vault
by a small animal. Kast sensed the Synth Flux was strong in the place, and while Mira thought the world might be a good base, Joren disagreed. Kast was apparently right, as the small animal led them to a group of legion vanguard who were dealing with animals of the same species, making it clear that the Dominion knew the world.
Along with Mira, Joren defeated the legion vanguard, who intended to sell the animals on the black market, and then left the planet to continue his mission to verify the planets assigned to him.
Lotho Minor
"
I'm not sure there's much to give up on, Mira. Unless we want to make a base out of scrap metal.
"
―Joren Kast to Dax Corbin
Akar Duel and his automatons confront Kast and the rebels.
At some point Joren, Mira, Dax, Gorath, and LQ-9M, were sent by the Alliance to investigate the planet
Lotho Minor
for a new base, traveling on the
Shadow Hawk
. Kast did not think there was anything worthwhile on the planet and that it would not lend well to a base, but Corbin warned him to keep a look out on the planet. Hearing strange noises, Kast activated his flux blade and warned the others. They were then approached by native automatons and a
Duros
named
Akar Duel
.
After Duel announced his plans to take the
Shadow Hawk
for himself with the help of the automatons
Teetee
,
Doc
and others, LQ-9M ran from the ship calling Kast's name and informing him that his initial scans had been incorrect and that there was life on Lotho Minor. After seeing that thee protocol automaton was acting without asking for permission or wearing a restraining bolt, the automatons of the planet turned on duel, allowing Kast and his friends to escape on the
Shadow Hawk
, and leaving them believing that the planet was not a good location for a new rebel base.
Kudo
"
That was good thinking, sending the automatons out with a cover story.
"
―Kast, to Corbin
Around this the rebellion eventually temporarily settled on the planet
Kudo
. During the holiday
Life Day
, Kast helped with a mission to stop the ISB from discovering them. After LQ-9M and DR-7X brought the
ISB agent
into a dark room, Kast held a pulse rifle to him and Gorath knocked him down. Afterward, Kast commended Corbin for coming up with the plan to use the automatons as a trap, but Corbin gave the credit to Gorath. Kast then watched a light display created by a
Life Day Orb
and the Coalition planned to leave the planet.
Cymoon 1
"
You, boy, are
no
Keeper. Who are you?
"
"
You killed my father!
"
"
I've killed very many fathers. You'll have to be more specific.
"
―Xarn Velgor and Joren Kast during a brief duel in Cymoon 1
Alongside Venn, Corbin, Gorath, DR-7X, and LQ-9M, Kast took part of a
mission
to infiltrate
Cymoon 1
. To do so, the rebel team hijacked the ship of an emissary sent by Jabba the Hutt to negotiate a weapons deal at
Weapons Factory Alpha
, with Corbin as its replacement and Venn and Kast disguised as his bodyguards.
Joren Kast frees a group of captive Dominion slaves held on the grounds of Weapons Factory Alpha on Cymoon 1.
Once inside the facility, they revealed themselves as rebels to
Overseer
Aggadeen
, took their escorts down and made their way to the central power station. While Venn, Corbin, and DR-7X rigged the place to destroy it, Kast watched for additional Dominion troops, using what little he knew of the Synth Flux to reach out and sense any nearby presence. However, he was drawn to caged
slaves
, whom he proceeded to free, and guided them to a rendezvous with Corbin and Venn, when they were alerted of Velgor's arrival by Gorath.
Determined to fight Velgor for killing his father and Malkor, he heard his mentor's voice speak to him from beyond, telling him to run, just as he faced the
Dark Lord
.
However, he was no match for Velgor, who quickly seized Kast's flux blade and was about to kill him, until he realized that Kast's flux blade had been once his own. Corbin then intervened with an
Colossus Strider
, crashing the
walker
through the factory's walls, giving Kast and the slaves an opening to escape from the facility.
Joren Kast is dispatched by Xarn Velgor following a brief confrontation on Cymoon 1.
Seeing so many of the freed slaves die by Dominion fire, Kast regretted his decision, but continued fighting after stealing a
speeder bike
. After the Coalition forces' walker opened fire against Velgor,
Kast picked his flux blade up from the rubble, rushed to assist his team—whose walker had fallen down due to Velgor—, and provided some cover fire. Determined to destroy the factory, Kast disobeyed Venn's orders and used his stolen speeder bike to destroy the power core despite Velgor's threat. Afterwards, he managed to board the
Shadow Hawk
, reunite with his team and escape Cymoon 1, yet with his morale at rock bottom.
Return to Vorath Prime
"
"There's nothing here for me now." That's what I said when I left this place. Let's hope I was
wrong
.
"
―Joren Kast
Once they returned to the
Alliance Fleet
, Kast continued working on his flux blade techniques until Venn came to see him. Kast told her that he lacked interest in undertaking any more missions, as he did not consider himself a Keeper but a danger, and that until he could figure out who he was, he could not be around the Alliance; he asked Venn to let him go. Much to her and Corbin's dismay, Kast (along with DR-7X) took off in an Starhawk and set off on a course for Vorath Prime. Hoping to find some guidance in Malkor's hut, Kast and R2 made their way across the
desert
to the abandoned residence, where they found it being pillaged by Tusken raiders, who fled in fear upon seeing his activated flux blade.
Kast duels Kael Ryn after being ambushed in Zeth Malkor's former home on Vorath Prime.
Inside the hut, Kast had R2 scan the ransacked place for anything of value, and the small android came across a box addressed to Kast. However, before they could find out its contents, one of
Kael Ryn
's
flash grenades
incapacitated DR-7X and blinded Kast.
Fett proceeded to aim a
rifle
to his head, but Kast
fought back
with his flux blade despite his temporary blindness. Despite his disadvantage and multiple wounds done by Fett, Kast managed to deflect the bounty hunter's shot at point-blank and Fett tackled him in anger.
Close to death by his own flux blade as he struggled against Fett, DR-7X saved him by hurling Malkor's box and knocking the hunter out. Still blinded and carrying the box, Kast had DR-7X lead him to his X-Wing.
On his way to an unknown location, he read some of Malkor's passages on his
journals
, including one set during the Great Drought involving him.
Showdown on Nar Shaddaa
"
You want to finally be a Keeper Knight, kid? Here's your chance. Go die like one.
"
―Kreel to Joren Kast
Though Malkor's journals contained a number of stories, Kast became frustrated as he continued to read them as he did not understand what lessons were to be learned about using a flux blade or wielding the Synth Flux. Though Drex suggested returning to the fleet, Kast had no intention of returning until he found the answers he was seeking. Drex's suggestion did, however, give the young Keeper an idea: to venture into the
Keeper Temple
on
Nexara City-world
, the former home of the Keepers of the Flux. Before he could travel to Nexara City-world, however, Kast set a course to
Nar Shaddaa
, a world of pirates and outlaws known as the "Smuggler's Moon." Upon landing, Kast set off looking for someone who could help him get to the
Keeper Temple
. He entered a cantina, where he was immediately attacked by criminals looking to rob and kill him. Kast brandished his flux blade and demanded that they cease their attack, while the bartender asked where he acquired a Keeper's flux blade. He did not answer, instead saying that he was simply on Nar Shaddaa to conduct business and had already stated his purpose for entering the establishment. The bartender guessed that Kast was seeking entry into the Keepers Temple, meaning he would need to hire someone who could sneak him onto the capital world of the Dominion. The bartender told him that he could do that for the boy, but the price would be the flux blade—a price that Kast rejected, so the bartender resorted to his previous plan of killing and robbing the boy.
Kast and the thief fall towards the streets of Hutta Town, as the young Keeper reaches for his weapon.
Before the others could kill him, however, a
thief
stole the flux blade and fled the bar. Kast gave chase,
pursuing the
thief
through the streets and over rooftops. The thief jumped onto a ship that was passing by and hung onto the bottom of it, and Kast used the Synth Flux to make the now-even longer jump towards them and grab the thief's leg. The thief lost his holding and both men fell towards the streets of
Hutta Town
below, where Kast retrieved his flux blade once both he and the thief landed. The young Keeper was then confronted by
Grakkus
the Hutt, a Nar Shaddaa crime lord who employed the thief that stole the flux blade. Kast fought the Hutt's
MagnaGuard
, while at the same time overhearing the thief telling Grakkus that Kast said his father was a Keeper and that he was seeking access to the Keepers Temple. Having his own plans for the boy, Grakkus knocked Kast out with a punch to the face.
Kast later awoke to find himself in Grakkus'
palace
, which contained untold numbers of relics from the Keepers of the Flux. Grakkus held out a Keeper
holocron
, though Kast did not know what it was. Grakkus, who explained that he was a collector of
Keeper artifacts
in addition to being a crime lord, told him that the item was a holocron and that only an individual who was strong with the Synth Flux could open it. Though Kast had no experience with holocrons, the Hutt gave him five seconds to open the holocron or be killed. Under threat of death, the boy reached out with the Synth Flux and was able to open the holocron, as well as the many others that were in the collection. They revealed themselves as containing historical records about the Keepers and the Shade Covenant, including the revelation that there was a
Keeper Temple
on
Vrogas Vas
. With Kast having proved himself, Grakkus believed he would make a fine addition to his collection as the last living Keeper. Grakkus planned to put Kast into an arena fight that he would bill as the death of the last Keeper, so the Hutt ordered him taken to the arena's gamemaster to be prepared. Drex, knowing that Kast had been captured, send a distress call to the Alliance Fleet, where it was intercepted by the High Command. Though Chancellor Mothma was unwilling to send a team of Coalition forces on a suicide mission despite Kast's importance to the Coalition, Gorath and Loquim—who remained with the fleet while Corbin and Venn were on their own mission—volunteered to rescue their captured friend.
Grakkus the Hutt's "Gamemaster" Kreel bests Kast in a training duel on Nar Shaddaa.
The gamemaster—secretly an Dominion legion vanguard named
Sergeant
Kreel
—introduced himself to Kast once the Keepers was led to the arena, and he stated that it was his job to make sure Kast was prepared to put on a good show before ultimately dying in the arena. Though Kast initially refused to fight, Kreel knew he would ultimately do so because he wanted to learn to hone his skills as a Keeper. The Kreel also told him that the Keepers Temple no longer existed, as it had been converted into the Dominion Palace—so whatever Kast was hoping to find was gone, as were the other Keeper temples in the stellar realm. He then threw the flux blade to Kast and removed the binds that the boy's hands were in. While surrounded by MagnaGuards, the Kreel used a flux blade of his own and engaged in a duel to begin preparing him for the arena match. Though the Kreel was not a Keeper, he was a more-skilled swordsman and quickly bested Kast. Kast then engaged multiple MagnaGuards in combat.
The big event soon arrived, and crime lords and other criminal types flocked to watch the death of the last Keeper. Kast spoke to Grakkus, who reminded him that no one wanted to watch him win and that everyone had paid to see him die, though the young Keeper believed he could succeed nonetheless. Grakkus also revealed that scavengers had found Kast's Starhawk and the Journal of Zeth Malkor, which Grakkus intended to add to his collection. The event began once Grakkus left, and Kast entered into the arena and was introduced as the last of the Keepers. The announcer claimed that he was a veteran of battles across the stellar realm and that he had fought Hutts and bounty hunters alike, hyping up the untrained Keeper hopeful. The announcer then introduced the Keepers's opponent:
Kongo
the Disemboweler, a large and fearsome creature from the
dolovite
mines
of
Mustafar
. Kast ignited the green-bladed flux blade that was given to him for the show and the fight began. The young Keeper slashed his blade at the creature, but the opponent's armor was resistant to a flux blade blade and made it difficult for Kast to combat the creature. He remained confident, however, reminding himself that he blew up the Void Core, fought Kael Ryn, and was trained by Zeth Malkor. He closed his eyes in an attempt to use the Synth Flux as his ally, but Kongo used the opportunity to throw Kast across the arena and knocking him onto the ground.
Kast battles Dominion soldiers in Grakkus' arena.
The beast grabbed Kast as the audience cheered for Kongo to make the kill, and Kast tried calling out to Malkor to ask for miracle. Just as he did so, the beast's
shock collar
was activated by Kreel, who revealed himself to Grakkus as an Dominion and announced he was taking Kast into custody. Legion Vanguard
poured
into the arena and surrounded Kast, arresting both him and the Hutt. Grakkus had one trick up his sleeve, though, and triggered an electromagnetic pulse that disabled the Imperials' pulse rifles. Grakkus, skilled at combat in his own right, began fighting the weaponless legion vanguard, while other troops grabbed Kast. Just then, the young Keeper saw that Drex, who had infiltrated the palace, recovered his flux blade and threw it into the arena, so Kast grabbed it and cut the soldiers down. Kongo chased the spectators out of the arena, and Kast while Kreel confronted Kast, knocking him to the ground and revealing his true identity as a legion vanguard of the
501st Legion
tasked with arresting him. Kast was saved by the timely arrival of Grakkus, who Kreel attacked as Kast got away.
Kast regrouped with Corbin, Venn, Gorath, and the automatons, all of whom ultimately traveled to Nar Shaddaa to rescue their captive friend. Corbin, Venn, and Gorath each wielded flux blades that Drex stole from the palace so they were not defenseless in the wake of the EMP blast, leading Kast to quip that Corbin looked good with a flux blade. Drex recovered Malkor's journal and Kast wanted to go back to the palace to recover more artifacts, but the arena was flooded with pulse rifle-wielding legion vanguard so Gorath grabbed the resistant Kast and brought him out of the arena. The Coalition forces returned to the
Shadow Hawk
and escaped from Nar Shaddaa.
Vrogas Vas
"
…right now I'm just a Coalition pilot who needs to help his friends. Learning to be a Keeper will have to wait.
"
―Kast, to DR-7X
Though he was unable to acquire the relics in Grakkus' palace, the holocrons he activated gave Kast one important piece of information: the existence of a Keeper Temple on Vrogas Vas. Kast returned to the Alliance but still sought answers, so he traveled to Vrogas Vas and was stationed in orbit with Red Squadron, along with
Blue Squadron
and
Yellow Squadron
. While in orbit and preparing for training exercises, Xarn Velgor
—who had learned of Kast's location from one of his associates, a rogue
archaeologist
named
Aphra
—dropped out of voidspace in his TIE Advanced x1 starfighter. Velgor made quick work of Blue Squadron and Yellow Squadron, destroying them both as Red Squadron looked on. Kast sensed that Velgor was the pilot of the enemy starfighter, made a run towards him, and crashed his Starhawk fighter into the Shade Lord's TIE Advanced. The collision damaged both vessels and send them hurtling towards the surface of Vrogas Vas.
Kast senses the presences of Zeth Malkor and Xarn Velgor in the temple ruins.
Kast and Velgor both survived the crashes of their starfighters, though they impacted on the surface in different places. The young Keeper crashed outside of the ruins of the Keepers Temple, from which he witnessed the destruction of
Gray Squadron
forces sent to attack Velgor—part of a major Coalition operation to capture the lone Shade Lord. He put off his search for answers and began to make his way towards the Coalition forces, determined to help his friends.
As he passed through the ruins of the Keepers Temple, he could see
yet another
Coalition pilot being killed by the Dark Lord. Unknown to Kast was that, at the same time, he was being tracked by Velgor's companions—Aphra and the
assassin automatons
BT-1
and
0-0-0
. While the Shade Lord's followers prepared a plan to capture the boy, Kast found writings in the ruins that were written with a flux blade, letting him deduce that the ruins were from the Keepers. A cold feeling passed over him and he could sense the presence of what he thought was Zeth Malkor and Xarn Velgor. He tried to concentrate, but he was interrupted by what he thought was Loquim's arrival. In reality, it was Triple-Zero, painted in gold by Aphra, who placed his metal hand on Kast and sent an electrical charge through the boy's body, knocking him unconscious.
Aphra and Triple-Zero prepared to take Kast hostage when Drex stepped in to help. Bee Tee then brought his arsenal to bear on the rebel's astromech and chased him off. Aphra and Triple-Zero then seized Kast but were stopped by the smuggler Dax Corbin, who had a sniper rifle aimed at them. Gorath then came in and attacked the protocol automaton, ripping his arm off. Kast then regained consciousness, dispatched Bee Tee, and sliced off Triple-Zero's other arm with his flux blade. Kast and his allies then retreated when Bee Tee fired his weapons blindly.
Kast, Corbin, Gorath, and Drex made their way back to the Shadow Hawk. They then lifted off in order to aid princess Mira. They were forced to the ground when their ship was crashed into by the Thoran bounty hunter Black Krrsantan. While Corbin and Gorath dealt with the mercenary, Kast left to head back to the Keepers ruins.
Joren arrived back at the the Keepers temple looking for Mira. It was then that he heard the voice of Zeth Malkor, who told him that he should not have come to this place and that he was not ready for what is coming. Joren was then intercepted by a company of legion vanguard who opened fire on him. He retreated behind cover but was hit in the head and knocked unconscious.
Joren was taken prisoner by the forces of Commander
Karbin
. However Xarn Velgor, who had a rivalry with Karbin, used the force to crash the shuttle Kast was kept in. Now free, he then caught up with Corbin, Gorath, LQ-9M, and DR-7X. They made their way back to the Shadow Hawk, however Dr. Aphra had laid out a minefield to stop them. When she was distracted she was knocked out from behind by Warden Mira. Believing her to be of use, Venn decided to take the archaeologist with them. As they departed Vrogas Vas, Kast felt remorse that he would not be able to go back to the temple and see what was hidden there.
Learning the ways of the smuggler
"
How else are we gonna replace the money we just lost?
"
"
Heh, don't worry, kid. I know just the thing. It's a big stellar realm. And believe me, there's always something somewhere in need of smuggling.
"
―Kast and Corbin
Kast and Corbin deliver nerfs on Ibaar.
Upon returning to the Coalition fleet with their new prisoner, Doctor Aphra, Kast and his friends went their separate ways; Venn, Drex, and Loquim went to detain Aphra at an Alliance-controlled space station,
Sunspot Prison
, with the assistance of
Sana Starros
, whom Venn hired to transport them to the prison. Meanwhile, Corbin and Kast were dispatched to buy supplies for the rebellion. Corbin attempted to double the money he was given for supplies by betting on
sabacc
. However, he was caught cheating, and had to escape with Kast on the
Shadow Hawk
.
Kast and Corbin traveled to Nar Shaddaa where Joren was tasked with finding a customer in need of a ship. He made a deal to haul
nerfs
to
Ibaar
for 5,000 credits, which was just enough to replace the credits lost by Corbin. Kast and Corbin were soon intercepted by Razor drones who suspected the Shadow Hawk of smuggling illegal livestock.
Joren was able to shoot the fighters and he and Corbin made it to Ibaar with the nerfs. While on Ibaar, Kast blackmailed Corbin into allowing him to fly the Shadow Hawk for a few parsecs by threatening to tell Mira about losing the credits in a Sabacc game.
After buying the supplies, Kast and Corbin received a distress call from Sana and arrived at Sunspot Prison. They were immediately knocked out and taken captive by the
masked assailant
who was attacking the prison. Neither came to until after the battle had already ended and the attacker had been captured.
Temporary outpost
"
I just go word that the cargo vessel just arrived. Looks like the mission was canceled. Hope you didn't go through too much trouble.
"
―Joren Kast to DR-7X
Sometime after the assault on Sunspot Prison, the Alliance established a temporary
outpost
on the Outer Rim planet
Reamma
. Venn gave Joren a mission to find a missing Alliance cargo vessel. Kast and DR-7X prepared to make the flight to look for them. Before they could do that, however, Venn informed Kast that the vessel reported in, cancelling the mission.
Hijacking the
Harbinger
"
Chances are...This is the last voyage of the
Harbinger
. So let's make it a memorable one.
"
―Kast to Venn and Corbin
Sometime later, Kast and Venn rendezvoused with rebel leadership. Admiral Ackbar, General Dodonna, and Mon Mothma presented them with the task of providing aid to the people of
Tureen VII
, a planet that had provided aid to the rebellion in the past. A heavily blockaded planet with Dominion bases on both of its moons and swarms of Razor drones in orbit, the Alliance could not risk sending the fleet. Joren and Mira proposed stealing an Dominion Dominion Cruiser and using it to infiltrate the blockade. Despite some reluctance from Dodonna, Ackbar and Mothma realized that it was the only choice left.
Skorii-Lei Rescue
Afer the hijacking of the
Harbinger
, Mira ended up grievously injured on the planet
Skorii-Lei
. Cared for by former engineer
Pash Davane
and carrying vital Alliance information, Venn was pursued by legion vanguard and barely managed to escape aboard the
Shadow Hawk
with Kast, Corbin, and Gorath. Aboard the
Shadow Hawk
, Davane provided Kast with Mira's data tape, which he noted was a sign of great trust between the two of them. With her place as a friend of the Alliance confirmed, Kast and his allies returned Davane to her home on Skorii-Lei.
Journey to Vagadarr system
"
You remind me of him. But more than that... you remind me of me. You... you're nothing but a frightened child, just like I was. Do you know the only thing I was good at... when I was a child? Walking away.
"
―Garro to Kast
Kast worked with Garro to revive a giant of living stone.
Despite the escape from the
Harbinger
, the heroes quickly realized that LQ-9M was captured during the commotion, and Kast was unable to stop DR-7X from commandeering an
X-Wing
to rescue the
protocol automaton
on his own. With his own Starhawk's
hyperdrive
temporarily disabled, he resumed reading Zeth Malkor's journal. Identifying a symbol in the journal as a
star map
, Kast decided to visit the
Vagadarr system
after reading of a Keeper Master who had ventured there in the past and encountered enormous
giants
made of living stone that gave those in close proximity a power known as
Stonepower
. Kast hoped that going there would give him the knowledge he needed to save DR-7X and LQ-9M.
Like the Keepers Master before him, Kast was able to navigate through the
planet
's heavy debris field and land on the surface, where he encountered
Garro
, the last remaining native human, who initially wanted to use Kast's life force to kill the few remaining giant children. Determined to protect them, Kast inspired Garro to change his mind and sacrifice his own life force to revive a dead giant. Kast and the giant children then ventured into the giant's cave to find the heart of the mountain and finish what Garro started in reviving the giant, thus completing the mission of the Master from years past and taking another step in his journey to becoming a
Keeper
.
Horox III
"
So, come, shake hands, have dinner with the nice lady, and we walk away with an actual sentient Ancient Keeper. You get your teacher, I get an eyewitness to the Ancient past."
"I'm not sure.."
"C'mon, are you out, farm boy? Or are you in, Keeper Knight?
"
―Aphra and Joren Kast on the visit to Ktath'atn
The bar on Horox III
Sometime later, as the Alliance was setting up a
temporary outpost
on
Horox III
, Kast went to a local
bar
. Threatened by its denizens for being a human, he was forced to defend himself against them despite his warnings that he would be able to defeat them in a fight. The fight was compounded when Doctor Aphra, who had lied to Sana Starros in order to find Kast, arrived and sparked the anger of the entire bar after saying Kast was with her, forcing Kast, Aphra, and Black Krrsantan to fight the entire bar. After the fight, Kast told Aphra he did not want her following him because he did not want to have anything to do with someone who worked for Velgor, but she caught his attention by saying she knew where to find a Keeper Master.
Kast first refused to admit he was trying to be a Keeper, Aphra explaining that she had figured it out from Velgor's vendetta and Kast's one-in-a-million shot to destroy the Void Core plus his general talent. She introduced him to the
Rur crystal
of the ancient Keeper splinter group known as the
Ordu Aspectu
with an ancient Keeper personality inside of it. By playing off Kast's hopes to be better with the Synth Flux and a true Keeper Knight, Aphra convinced an initially hesitant Joren to
visit
the
Screaming Citadel
with her, explaining that that introducing him to the
Queen of Ktath'atn
as a Keeper would convince the Queen to reactive the crystal for them. On the way there, Kast reaquainted himself with 0-0-0 and BT-1, remembering the former for electrocuting him and the latter from R2 kicking him. Kast lamented not telling Corbin, Venn, or Starros about where he was going, but he and Aphra both agreed they would have tried to stop them and they had no time to waste.
The temporary Coalition Outpost on Horox III.
Meanwhile, at the Coalition Outpost on Horox III, Corbin then enlisted Starros' help in communicating with Kast's temporary
R5
astromech automaton
S4
, who could only speak
Binary
and thus was not easily able to communicate with Venn. Venn asked the automaton for Kast's whereabouts but the automaton responded that he could not tell. Questioning the automaton, Starros learned that Kast did not go by himself but that he went with Doctor Aphra. An unhappy Mira and Corbin wondered why Kast would go with Aphra, Venn suggesting she kidnapped Kast while Corbin and Starros suspect she could have reprogrammed the automaton to keep a secret.
The Screaming Citadel
The Queeen of Ktath'atn
"
I know every sort of creature you can image, Doctor. And many I'm sure you cannot. They have all been paraded through the halls of this citadel. All by people desperate to gain my favor. But never once has anyone brought me a Keeper
"
―The Queen of Ktath'atn to Kast and Aphra
Kast journeyed to the Screaming Citadel with the hope of learning more about the Keepers.
After arriving on Ktath'atn, Kast and the others encountered a mob of villagers. Kast wants to help feed them and Aphra just calls him adorable and has Krrsantan scare them off. After making the trek to the the entire party, except for Krrsantan were allowed into the citadel. Kast was curious how Aphra met Krrsantan but she just said it was a long story and that she owed him a lot of money. Kast then asked how Aphra had found him. Noticing he was struggling, Aphra helped Kast put his
Spavat
on to meet the Queen, and after finished he insisted that he was more than just a farm boy. While walking toward the main hall, 0-0-0 teased Kast for stuttering when Aphra commented on her attractiveness but she told the automaton only she was allowed to tease him.
In the ballroom, Kast was mesmerized by the crowd. Aphra told Kast to follow her lead and advised him to hide his flux blade, but implies he is used to keeping his Keeper identity, which he hopes to solidify, a secret. When Kast asked about the Keepers in the crystal, Aphra said she only met him briefly but that he had strong opinions. After encountering an
Ezaraan
guest, Kast was initially excited by them wanting to take down the Dominion, but after they explained they wanted to replace it with a
Dominion of the Ezaraa
where they would consume the flesh of all "lesser species," Kast expressed that he thought the guests were crazy. The two soon saw the
Queen of Ktath'atn
, and after being welcomed by one of her servants,
Varroa
, Aphra presented Kast, describing him as a very interesting Vorath Prime farm boy, but was rejected. When the Ezaraan mocked Kast, Aphra defended him, but the Ezaraan grabbed her arm. Kast warned the Ezaraan to leave Aphra alone and used the Synth Flux to throw the Ezaraan to the ground, intriguing the Queen and earning a compliment from Aphra. Varroa ordered all the guests to leave but allowed Kast and his companion to stay so that the Queen could hear his request in the morning, Aphra telling him he did great. Later, the Queen told her servant
Vespinax
that Kast's power and little experience made him perfect for her intentions to eat him. She then grew her power in anticipation of eating the Keepers.
The next morning, Kast and Aphra made their way to a dining hall to have breakfast with the Monarch. On the way, after Kast expressed how creepy he thought the place was, Aphra assured him that the Queen's obsession with Keeper would get her to do what they wanted, but Skwalker was worried by this because of his encounter with Grakkus the Hutt and Aphra's mention that the Queen "keeps" her oddities. Aphra told Kast to let her do the talking and then the two walked inside and sat at a table with the Queen, Aphra introducing the both of them. Excited to see a Keeper, but wanting more verification, the Queen commanded Kast to move a nearby dining tray with the Synth Flux, though he was unable to until the Queen's servant,
Vespinax
, threatened him with a knife to his throat. However, moving the tray revealed that under it were
Abersyn symbiotes
that the Queen planned on implanting them with, so after Aphra set off an explosion that knocked them both down they quickly made an attempt to escape while Aphra explained to Kast what the symbiotes were. Before they could escape, in the Citadel's hallway, they were nearly stopped by many of the Queen's thralls.
The Abersyn hive
"
You're not a Keeper. You just know what a Keeper knows. That's not enough. Knowing isn't enough. Being a Keeper is about
being
. Letting go…feeling…and more than anything…the Keepers feel free.
"
―Kast, to Eternal Rur
The Queen of Ktath'atn goes after Kast.
After attempting to run, Kast prepared to use his flux blade to defend them, but Corbin, Venn, and Starros arrived at their location on Aphra's ship, the
Ark Angel II
, with her automatons 0-0-0 and BT-1 in tow. Starros attempted to shoot Aphra, but Kast blocked the blast with his flux blade, claiming that she was a good person and his friend, he willingly went with her, and he knew what he was doing, despite Venn's attempts to call his decision unwise and threats to court-martial him if he did not follow orders. Meanwhile, the Queen's servants and armed security guards pursued the group, so they escaped into an Abersyn symbiote laboratory, which Kast decided people needed to know about. There, Kast and Aphra got into an argument about the merits of the Dominion in which Aphra revealed her mother was killed in the Gene Wars, claimed that regular people preferred the Dominion's peace to war, and insisted that she was not like Corbin as Kast thought. She attempted to mock him for his upbringing until Kast revealed everyone on his farm was killed by the Dominion. Aphra led Kast to a nearby room and sold him out to the Queen of Ktath'atn, who promptly mesmerized him to capture him. Aphra warned the Queen not to do the same to her too or she would blow up charges she placed in Kast's pockets. Venn, Corbin, and Starros then saw Kast get captured, the former calling out to him and later refusing to leave him behind.
The Queen connected Kast to a machine that would allow her to directly feed on him. As Aphra left with the activated Rur crystal, she asked what would happen to Kast, and the Queen revealed she would not infect him with a symbiote, but started to drink his life force to increase her power. Upon speaking to to the crystal outside, Rur revealed to Aphra that she felt guilt over leaving Kast behind and felt guilt over blaming her actions on her tragedy, when Kast's tragedy made him a good person. Corbin attempted to rescue him, but was quickly enthralled and forced to lead the Queen's forces, as Venn and Starros' desperation to save Kast lead them to place a symbiote into Krrsantan despite Venn's misgivings. Kast eventually tore free of the machine and in the ensuing struggle managed to re-arm himself with his flux blade. While the Queen attacked him with her powers, Aphra bombed the room and came inside, revealing that she had scaled the Citadel and was there to rescue Kast.
