Kast explained that he had never heard of that happening but thought it could potentially be possible. When the Scourge then asked if Kast's cybernetic arm felt connected to the Synth Flux, he explained that his whole being felt connected, but that he was not a true cyborg like Xarn Velgor. Kast expressed his concern that something was wrong with LQ-9M, leading to the Scourge briefly considering killing him. Instead, the automaton just apologised and agreed to get a diagnostic, before agreeing to Kast's request to send DR-7X his way.
As the scourge of the automatons continued, Calrissian recruited Kast and Venn for a mission to the planet
Epikonia
to rescue Lobot, who had been infected by the Scourge. When they arrived in the
Shadow Hawk
, Kast was unsure what made the world special until Venn explained that it was a communications hub. Calrissian then refused to allow Kast to access transpoder signals and turned off the
Shadow Hawk
'
s navigation systems to prevent the infection from reaching the ship's
automaton core
. Kast and Venn were concerned, but Calrissian managed to land them safely on a satelite dish. The Scourge soon learned that Kast was on the planet and became obsessed with the idea of infecting him so that it could use the Synth Flux.
Kast, Venn, Calrissian, and Gorath fight the Scourge on Epikonia.
As the group traversed through the planet's main city where the Scourge was headquartered, Kast noticed that there were no people around. They were then
attacked
by a horde of violent Scourge-infected automatons, Kast using his yellow flux blade to begin taking them down alongside his friends.
Kast sliced through the ever-growing mob of automatons while urging himself to stay calm, focused, and concentrated on the Synth Flux. When the automatons ominously and suddenly retreated, a new swarm of
spider-automatons
attacked. Kast tried to fight them off, but was quickly infected with the Scourge by one. As the Scourge was controlling Kast's body and declared itself the 'new Flux' it was defeated by Ajax Sigma, freeing Kast from its control. Though unsure what happened, Kast went with the others to find Lobot. Once they did, Calrissian and Gorath started to take him back to the ship while Kast and Venn assisted people who had been experimented on by the Scourge.
Kast and Venn returned to the ship very quickly, as they found themselves unable to assist a great amount. They discovered Calrissian with the
Talky automaton
that contained much of the Alliance's secrets. As Venn and Calrissian threatened each other, Kast urged them to focus on healing Lobot. Calrissian then explained the circumstances that had led him to sell the automaton, and thus the Alliance's secrets, to Jabba the Hutt, leaving Kast and Venn shocked– Venn arresting Calrissian for these crimes.
The Shade Covenant and the Kast
"
Being healed doesn't mean you're weak. Here... I'll show you.
"
―Kast, to Gol
In order to learn more about his enemy, the Shade Covenant, Kast tracked down Gretta in the Outer Rim with the help of DR-7X and enlisted her help in once again attempting to purify the Shade Lord Gol's corrupted kyber crystal. The two were also assisted by
Feez
, Gretta's aunt. Feez told Kast that to heal the crystal, he would need to journey into the memories of its corrupter and understand their pain in order to succeed. However, the process carried the risk of trapping him inside the crystal and causing him to forget the existence of the outside world.
As Gretta and Feez began the ritual, Kast's mind entered the crystal. He found himself falling from the sky into Gol's castle hall. Like before, Gol stated that Kast was not welcome and would be removed. Kast refused and ignited his flux blade. In response, the entire court ignited their Shade Covenant flux blades. Kast knocked down a large number of Shade Covenant courtiers with the Synth Flux. However, with a single twitch of his finger, Gol Flux-lifted Kast into the air and slammed him onto the ground, knocking him out cold.
Gol ordered his soldiers to bring Kast into the dungeon. As Kast awoke, Gol stated that while Kast was a Keeper, he was unusual: he was clean, not wearing rags, and had a flux blade. He told Kast about the last Keeper he had in the court, the
Jester Keeper
, who eventually committed suicide to escape his torment, and stated that he intended to do the same to Kast. When Kast asked when was the vision taking place, Gol simply stated that it was now. When Kast offered to heal Gol's pain once again, Gol ordered his soldiers to beat him, stating that the Keepers was confused by "strange fantasies" and needed to see the reality: the Keepers could save no one in this time, not even themselves, and all they could do was suffer.
Gol, the Shade Covenant that Kast found inside the red crystal.
After the beating was finished, Gol told Kast that no one could leave behind their pain, and that it was pain that defined him since his earliest days, looking to an infant crying in a crib in the cell next to Kast's. Kast once again offered to help, asking if Gol wanted the pain to stop. Gol replied that pain never stops as it is people's history, he pointed out that Kast's hand was severed and would always be severed, causing his cybernetic hand to spontaneously disappear. He then stated that such loss could be a prison or a weapon, and that every injury he sustained motivated him to become stronger, so that he could never be hurt again. At this point, he asked for Kast's name, when Kast replied, Gol made the same offer to Kast as when they first met: to combine Kast's pain with his, creating a better version of the vision. He stated that all Kast needed to see things his way was more pain.
After Gol left, Kast destroyed his prison door with the Synth Flux. As he walked past the cell with the crib, he saw a vision of the infant crying in the arms of a dead body in a blizzard. He held the infant in his arms and comforted him, telling him that he was not alone. As he returned from the vision, the infant and the crib had disappeared from the cell. Realizing that the infant was a past version of Gol, Kast closed his eyes and began walking through the dungeon. He saw visions of Gol being shunned and tormented throughout his early life, which eventually drove him to the deep flux. He comforted Gol throughout his life, shielding him from hurt and pain, convincing him that he was not alone, and showing him that the deep flux was not the only way.
Eventually, the adult Gol reappeared and attacked Kast with flux blade drawn, demanding Kast to stop making him weak. Kast stated that being healed does not make him weak and ignited his flux blade, but was surprised to see that its blade had turned from yellow to green. As the two duelled, Gol asked Kast who did he think he was. Kast stated that he was a Keeper, and Gol stated that he was a fool, like all who followed that path. Kast then disarmed Gol of his flux blade and stated that all paths lead in both directions. Though Gol was defeated, he stated that this place was built from pain, and to heal it, Kast would need to face his own pain. He then transformed into Xarn Velgor, and the dungeon transformed into the reactor shaft on Cloud City.
Instead of fighting Velgor, Kast flipped over him and ran deeper into Cloud City. As he ran, the
viewscreens
on the wall displayed all the pain and loss he had suffered: the deaths of Owen and Beru Lars, the death of Zeth Malkor, the loss of friends like Biggs Darklighter and Dax Corbin, being attacked by a Wampa on Kryos, and the death of his birth mother, Padmé Amidala. At this point, Velgor appeared before him, saying there was no escape.
Kast purifies the kyber crystal.
Kast agreed that there was no escape from their fated confrontation. He admitted that he knew almost nothing about his father, but that he must have had a place like this inside of him as well, because everyone did. He stated that his father did not begin as Xarn Velgor, and that he was once someone else, causing Velgor to morph into a shining silhouette of Xarn Velgor as a Keeper. Joren stated that no one is beyond help and touched Anakin's chest. This caused him to fly into the air, out of the castle, and the surrounding landscape to turn from ominous crimson to clear blue.
As Kast awoke from his vision, Gol's crystal was successfully healed, turning pure white. He then gave the crystal to Feez, saying he no longer needed it. When Gretta asked Kast what he learned about the Shade Covenant, Kast stated that the Shade Covenant he saw were from a long time ago and different from the ones today. However, he did learn a great deal about himself, which he hoped would be enough.
Trials and tribulations
"
I'd like to say something. I'm
Joren Kast
. I'm a...I'm studying the Synth Flux, and what advocate Georgio is saying is
impossible
.
"
―Joren Kast, during the trial of Lando Calrissian
After being arrested for selling Coalition secrets, Lando Calrissian called in the help of
lawyer
Salli Georgio
for his trial on
Home One
, and Joren Kast sat in the chamber during the trial, viewing witness questioning and testimonies. At one point during the trial, Georgio implied that Lando, in committing his crimes, may have been under the influence of a Keeper mind trick, as he was a known associate to Kast, a Keeper. Kast stood up and denied the possibility of this happening, citing his education on the Synth Flux and its inner workings.
Joren Kast was present for the trial of Lando Calrissian aboard
Home One
.
As the trial reached a conclusion, Kast and the rest witnessed Mon Mothma's hologram react to what was happening in the remote location she was broadcasting from. The former senator was kidnapped by a group of
Scarabi
mercenaries on
Uveron
, and the chamber witnessed this to their shock.
In response to this, Coalition command issued
Tactical Plan Eighteen
, dividing the fleet, while Kast continued to watch the trial, which then called Mira to the stand. Mira explained her knowledge of Calrissian's character, including his preparing the trap for Joren at Cloud City.
Soon after, Kast attended a meeting in
Home One'
s amphitheater where he, along with Venn, Ackbar, and
Crix Madine
viewed a ransom hologram of Mon Mothma. Mon's captor explained that she was being held at an undisclosed location, and would be released to the Coalition if they were to transfer two hundred million credits to their account within the next day. If they didn't, the captor said that the Dominion surely would. Ackbar said that this amount would cripple the Coalition, and Kast added that, conversely, Mothma being turned over to the Dominion would also destroy the Coalition due to ISB interrogation revealing all she knew. But it turned out that Calrissian recognized the voice of Mothma's captor as
Hachi
, a mercenary he once knew,
leading to his participation in a rescue mission, and eventually the clearing of his name, all of which Joren Kast witnessed.
Closing loose ends
"
You can't know that. Even the
Flux
can't predict the future. It's always shifting, changing.
"
―Joren Kast comforting his sister on
Home One
Joren Kast comforts a crying Mira Venn as she was overwhelmed with her Coalition duties.
Later, still on
Home One
, Kast was called to Venn's chamber at the princess's request, after she'd had a nightmare about Elara once again. Venn was on the verge of giving up on everything except for rescuing Corbin. Kast reassured her about her dedication to the cause, and Venn then explained her dreams, and how they express the guilt she still holds over Elara's destruction at the hands of Tarkin. Kast reminded her that she was in an impossible position and was not responsible, but Venn shifted the conversation to the next Void Core, blaming her dreams on that looming threat. Venn then broke into tears over the stress and turmoil of balancing it all, and Kast comforted her, relating to the feeling. Mira told Joren that he was one of the few people she could be a real person around, and thanked him. But then, Kast and Venn looked to the Coalition leader's comm unit as the voice of Evaan Verlaine came through: she had news about Elara and needed Mira in the hangar.
The two met the pilot in the ship's hangar, and Verlaine remarked that she hoped to fly with Kast again soon, with which he agreed. Evaan then told the two that the Alderaanian
survivor fleet
, a flotilla of refugees from Elara, had missed their last three check-ins. She was asking for permission to go investigate, which Venn approved– until Kast insisted that she herself go. Venn refused, but was eventually convinced that it was a military matter as the flotilla was technically considered Alliance reserves. Kast agreed to go as well, and the three set off, Venn and Kast in a
Taylander shuttle
and Verlaine in her Starhawk.
Joren Kast fights off the raiders on the
Thranta
to defend Mira Venn.
Deep in space, the group traveled as Verlaine explained that the Alderaanian survivor fleet could be taken out in a single strike, which Kast remarked was a tough way to live. They then came upon the
Thranta
, an Alderaanian vessel that was unresponsive to comms and giving the life sign of only one or two people. Kast and Venn docked with the vessel while Verlaine remained outside to watch their backs. The inside of the
Thranta
was dark, only lit by emergency lights, which Kast surmised was because the ship ran out of fuel and was abandoned. He and Venn followed the life sign to a chamber with a bacta tank in its center. Venn immediately recognized the woman as
Jora Astane
, a preserver of Alderaanian culture who was part of the flotilla. Kast, however, sensed that Astane was dead, making the presence of a life sign extra confusing. Joren then discovered that the machinery Astane was hooked up to was keeping her blood pumping enough for her neural activity to register on life sign scans, something Mira realized made her bait. Just then, Venn's comms buzzed again with the voice of Verlaine announcing that raiders had dropped out of voidspace and were firing on her and the
Thranta
. Kast and Venn ran to their shuttle, but the smaller, docked ship was fired upon by the raiders, destroying their only escape route. This blasted Venn into a wall, leading to Kast having to fight off the now boarding raiders with his flux blade. Using both his blade and the Synth Flux , Joren fought off the raiders and blocked their entrance, using the bought time to carry Mira to an escape pod. He then placed her inside and launched the pod from a panel on the wall, saving Venn's life as the
Thranta
completely detonated. Venn watched on in horror, as Kast escaped in another pod unbeknownst to Mira,
who'd been captured by the raiders– led by the long-thought-dead and now-cyborg Ellian Zahra.
Joren Kast duels Ellian Zahra.
On her
Raider-class corvette
, Zahra commanded her crew to use a grappling claw to grab Kast's escape pod, bringing him just close enough to their ship's hangar to be visible, but not bringing him onboard. Zahra had planned to kill Kast in front of Venn, but the Keepers ignited his flux blade inside the pod, slashing it from multiple angles, and exposing him to the vacuum of space. But using his Flux-abilites, Kast pulled himself inside the hangar. There, he faced the masses of Zahra's crew, deflecting pulse rifle bolts and causing chaos to buy time for reinforcements to arrive with Verlaine. Furious, Zahra wanted to kill Kast herself and drew her blade to do so before getting slammed in the back by Venn. Zahra stabbed Venn in the shoulder in response, and her automaton informed her that the ship was too damaged to escape– causing her crew to run and abandon ship in their individual vessels, leaving Joren without an opponent. In a rage, Zahra lunged at Kast, but the Keepers easily dodged or blocked her strikes, eventually slicing her blade in half, just as
Home One
and a squad of Starhawks arrived outside. Zahra called Kast "scum", and ran to her
Cumulus-class fighter
to escape. Kast checked on Venn, and told her that he let Zahra go in case she learned from this.
Home One
contacted Venn asking if they had her authorization to fire on Zahra. Venn said not unless she fired at them, and Zahra subsequently turned around, aiming her vessel at Kast and Venn. The princess then gave the order to fire, killing Zahra, as Kast told her that Zahra chose her own fate.
Zastiga
"
Weapons and war don't make you a Keeper. I know that now.
"
―Joren Kast
After the command of Red Squadron was transferred to Antilles following his promotion to squadron leader, which pleased Kast,
he declined to rejoin the squadron and chose to be stationed onboard the
Redemption
. When he went to
Zastiga
, he and DR-7X were the ones to greet Venn on her arrival to the planet after she and LQ-9M left the
Mellcrawler
, piloted by its owner
Nien Nunb
and Lieutenant
Caluan Ematt
. When the time came for Venn to attend the top clearance meeting with the Coalition leadership, Kast and Nunb met with Antilles for a discussion of "reconnaissance tactics" about Antilles' refusal to pay the
Sullustan
a drink. He would later return to escort Venn to her quarters before going to his, and two days later, he saw her off, before catching his own shuttle back to the
Redemption
.
Soon afterwards, he would go to
Kothlis
, where he would learn of Corbin's location—still frozen in carbonite and kept in Jabba the Hutt's palace—, and where he would keep himself up-to-date with the status of the Alliance Fleet's assembly. He had already devised a plan to rescue Corbin when Venn contacted him following the successful
Operation Yellow Moon
, and showed eagerness to join him on his attempts to
rescue
the Corellian smuggler.
Dominion Refining Platform M36
"
We just took an
Dominion refinery
without a
scratch
-- all thanks to
Kast
. I heard the stories. But I never saw anyone swing a
flux blade
up close like that.
"
―a Pau'an Coalition Major about Joren Kast
Joren Kast during the raid at Dominion Refining Platform M36.
Sometime before the rescue of Corbin, Kast took part in a
raid
on
Dominion Refining Platform M36
. During the raid, Kast used
his new flux blade
, along with his Flux abilities, to clear a path for Coalition forces to acquire copious Dominion fuel stores. His Coalition companions became awed at his use, although one of them, the
major
in charge of the operation, held reservations about its power as he had only previously seen such power being wielded by the Shade Covenant. Unknown to Kast, the Overlord had begun to probe his mental defenses from afar, which caused Kast to briefly open his saber in alarm.
As Kast and the strike team flew away from the platform, they were ambushed by an Dominion Dominion Cruiser, which launched several Razor Drones in pursuit. As the battle began to rage, Kast's mind was penetrated by a vision from the Overlord. The vision saw Joren follow a possible series of events wherein he would begrudgingly leave the Coalition strike team to its fate and crash-land on an unknown planet, losing his Starhawk in the process. As he journeyed from the wreckage together with DR-7X, Joren would leave his flux blade and helmet on the ground, symbolizing his complete retreat from the concerns of the war.
Having later broken free of the Overlord's vision, Kast proceeded to aid the Coalition forces in escaping the Dominion ambush, using his flux blade to prevent the major's shuttle from leaving, giving him time to pilot the shuttle and draw the Imperials away from the rest of the Coalition forces.
The last of the Keepers
Corbin's rescue
"
I am taking Captain Corbin and his friends. You can either profit by this or be destroyed. It's your choice, but I warn you not to underestimate my powers.
"
―Joren Kast, to Jabba Desilijic Tiure
In
4 ABY
,
Kast set in motion the plan to rescue Corbin from Jabba the Hutt
due to their failure to do so during the
Attack on the
Executor
.
Once again, Kast returned to Vorath Prime, and to Malkor's hut, wherein he found the missing parts he needed for the construction of his own flux blade, the pieces left to him by Malkor. While his friends bustled about preparing for the rescue, Kast retreated into the solitude of a desert cave they had chosen as their rendezvous point. Using the Synth Flux to assemble the weapon, he brought the kyber crystal inside to life, making the weapon his own, almost an extension of himself.
Kast returned to Vorath Prime in order to rescue Corbin from Jabba the Hutt, but ended up facing his rancor, Pateesa.
Afterward, Kast waited to see if Venn's impersonation of
Boushh
and liberation of Corbin was successful without his intervention, but she was only half-successful. She then sent a message to Kast and Calrissian that she had been captured as well, prompting Kast to act and go to Jabba's Palace.
Skillful use of the Synth Flux allowed Kast to get past Jabba's
Gamorrean
guards and use a Keeper mind trick to get his
majordomo
,
Bib Fortuna
, to present him to the Hutt. However, Jabba was not so easily swayed by Kast and ignored both his demand to give him Corbin and Gorath and Kast's warnings about underestimating him. Jabba laughed and dispatched Kast through a trap door where he was standing, dropping the young Keeper into a pit below, where the Hutt unleashed his massive
rancor
Pateesa
. Kast struggled against the terrifying creature that tried to feast on him. But to the shock of Jabba and his thugs, Kast was able to kill the monster by smashed the control panel with the
skull
- belonging to
Bidlo Kwerve
- dropping a large metal gate on its neck, upsetting
Malakili
, the rancor's keeper. Jabba was furious and sentenced all the captured Alliance heroes—including an unfrozen Corbin—to death in the
Great Pit of Carkoon
to be eaten by the
sarlacc
that resided there.
Jabba's dancers, including
Rystáll Sant
and
Lyn Me
, were ecstatic that Kast killed the beast and told
musician
Max Rebo
about it, Me believing and fantasizing that he had done it to avenge
Oola
, another dancer who had been fed to the creature, though dancer
Greeata Jendowanian
acknowledged that was not true.
Kast was taken on a
skiff
to the pit, where Jabba watched on from his
sail barge
the
Khetanna
. Before he was walked out onto a plank above the mouth of the sarlacc, Kast yelled to Jabba, telling him that he had one last chance to free them and would die if he did not. Kast nodded to Calrissian, who was on the skiff, and then saluted to DR-7X, who was on the
Khetanna
, before jumping off the plank, grabbing onto it, and using as a springboard to flip back onto the skiff. Kast then escaped when DR-7X shot his flux blade to him, allowing him to take down the guards and free the other prisoners on the
skiff
. A skirmish ensued in which Kast protected his friends, including facing and fending off Kael Ryn, jumping over and onto the
Khetanna
, ending the life of several of Jabba's thugs
—including
Klaatu
and
Nysad
, who shot his cyborg hand during the skirmish— and helping Venn, who had killed the Hutt by strangling him, escape the
Khetanna
by swinging together on a rope back over to the skiff. After destroying the sail barge by pointing one of its guns at its deck and activating it before they left it, he and his friends left on the skiff once he made sure they brought LQ-9M and DR-7X back with them.
Kast and his entire team then escaped unscathed through a dangerous sandstorm,
after which he boarded his Starhawk with DR-7X and parted ways with the others.
Keeping the promise
"
Joren… when gone am I, the last of the Keepers, will you be. Joren… the Synth Flux runs strong in your family. Pass on what you have learned. Joren… There… is… another… Sk-… Sky… walker…
"
―Vorn's last words, to Joren Kast
Keeping the promise, Kast returns to Myrkon, where he joins Vorn as he becomes one with the Synth Flux.
Kast then returned to Myrkon to complete his training and keep the promise he had made to Vorn,
but the aged Keeper Master, whose long life was supported by the Synth Flux, had fallen ill and was finally starting to succumb to age. Vorn explained this to Kast and assured him that it was the way of the Synth Flux, with no way to prevent it. Vorn told Kast that he would already completed all the training he required, but in order to finally become a true Keeper, he needed to confront Xarn Velgor one more time.
As his final moments creeped up on him, Vorn reflected on Kast being too old for what he was learning, too serious, too tall to fit in the hut, truly good, and that he was able to see Anakin's eyes in Joren's.
Kast inquired if Velgor was truly his father.
Having grown less and less sure since the revelation, Kast feared that it was the truth.
Kast received such confirmation from Vorn,
which angered him that they had lied to him for so long but Vorn confessed that he knew that Kast had not been ready to shoulder the burden of being the child of the Dark Lord of the Shade Covenant.
When Vorn suggested that Kast should not have rushed to face Velgor, Kast expressed his regret that he did not listen to him the first time. Vorn then reached out to Kast in the Synth Flux and felt his fear and awe.
The dying Keeper then warned him not to underestimate the Overlord's
powers
or allow the deep flux to take hold of him, and that as "the last of the Keepers" he had to pass on what he had learned.
Wishing to give Kast a chance to find connection and family in someone other than Velgor,
Vorn made one final revelation to Kast before he became one with the Synth Flux—that there was "another Kast."
Reunion with Malkor
"
Your father was seduced by the deep flux of the Synth. He ceased to be Xarn Velgor and became Xarn Velgor. When that happened, the good man who was your father was destroyed. So what I told you was true, from a certain point of view.
"
―Zeth Malkor to Joren Kast
Kast received confirmation from Malkor that Xarn Velgor was indeed his father.
Sensing his turmoil,
the spirit of Malkor appeared before Kast, who confronted him for lying about his father. Claiming that the good man that Xarn Velgor had been had died upon his fall to the deep flux, Malkor stated that he did not directly lie to him about his father; he was simply telling him what happened "from a certain point of view." Kast voiced his belief that there was still good within his father, and that he could not bring himself to kill him, but Malkor insisted that he needed to face him again in order to restore balance to the Synth Flux. Kast then asked about the "other Kast," who Malkor told him was his long-lost twin sister, who had been separated from him at birth to protect them from the wrath of the Dominion. Kast instantly came to the realization that this sister was Venn, which was confirmed by Malkor.
Kast almost immediately inquired about their mother's identity and fate, but Malkor asked him to let that truth stay with him for a while longer, as he knew Kast carried too many burdens already.
Adventure on the moon
"
General, count me in.
"
"
I'm with you too!
"
―Mira Venn and Joren Kast agreeing to join the team
Kast left his allies on Veldara, believing he endangered the mission.
Kast nevertheless accompanied his friends on the mission to the forested
moon
of
Veldara
to destroy the
shield generator
that protected the
DS-2 Void Core II Mobile Battle Station
in
orbit
, volunteering immediately upon arrival at the Coalition briefing above
Sullust
. As they approached the moon on the stolen
Lambda
-class Dominion shuttle
Tydirium
, Kast sensed Velgor's presence on the nearby
Super Dominion Cruiser
Executor
and realized his presence was endangering the mission; yet, it was his presence that prompted Velgor to allow their landing. Upon doing so, the Coalition force was discovered by a small force of
Dominion scout troopers
that were guarding the shield bunker, and Kast and Venn pursued them on
speeder bikes
. Though they successfully stopped the scouts, Venn became separated from Kast.
Upon learning of her disappearance, Corbin, Kast, Gorath, and the automatons went looking for her, only to be captured by a tribe of
Moorlings
. Using the Synth Flux to prove LQ-9M's
deific
"
magic
," Kast convinced the Moorlings to release them and, after LQ-9M's tale of their adventures, the whole group was accepted as a part of the
village
.
However, Kast felt as Velgor reached out to him through the Synth Flux and landed on the moon,
and knew that he had to confront Velgor. He quietly slipped away from the celebration, but Venn noticed that he had left and followed him. He informed her of what he was planning to do and also told her that, in the event that he did not make it back, she would the last hope of the Alliance, going on to tell her that she was his sister, and she could use the Synth Flux too.
Venn was initially shocked that she and Kast were actually brother and sister, but admitted to him that, somehow, she would always known, and attempted to dissuade him from confronting their father, but Kast was adamant that he could redeem Velgor, and set off.
Eventually encountering an Dominion patrol and willingly surrendering himself
to Commander
Igar
,
Kast was brought to Velgor and tried to convince his father to renounce the deep flux by referring to him as "Xarn Velgor," but Velgor harshly denounced his former name.
Although he managed to make Velgor question the dark truths that had long ruled him,
Kast ultimately became a prisoner of the Overlord as he was escorted to his
throne room
onboard the Void Core.
The final duel
"
If you will not be turned, you will be
destroyed
! Young fool. Only now, at the end, do you understand. Your feeble skills are no match for the power of the deep flux. You have paid the price for your lack of vision! Now, young Kast…you will die.
"
―Draven Nul to Joren Kast
Draven Nul retained a consistent belief that Kast would become his new apprentice and replace Velgor, even making the claim to a spectre of Vorn that appeared before him.
As Kast was brought before the Overlord, the Dark Lord of the Shade Covenant repeatedly told the young Keeper that he would fall to the deep flux just as easily as Velgor had, and let him know that he had purposely allowed the Alliance to discover the second Void Core's location in order to lure them into a trap and finish them off once and for all.
Kast confronted Velgor on the second Void Core, believing he could redeem his father in spite of the Overlord's control over him.
In order to temper Kast's negative emotions, Sidious forced him to watch as the
Dominion Fleet
engaged
the Coalition forces. He constantly encouraged Kast to give into his anger and hatred. Though Kast was resistant at first, his feelings of hopelessness and desperation only grew as the destruction of the Coalition fleet and the death of his friends appeared imminent. Attempting to strike down the Overlord, Kast found himself once again facing his father in a duel. Unlike their previous encounter on Bespin (where Velgor was largely able to dominate the proceedings, throughout), this time the pair would fight as equals.
Kast attempted to reason with Velgor in an attempt to redeem him to the bright flux of the Synth.
After successfully parrying a flurry of blows, Kast sent Velgor tumbling down a stairwell before regaining his composure and appealing to the good in his father. Velgor would have none of it and attacked once again, though eventually Kast withdrew from combat altogether and refused to fight Velgor any further, retreating into the shadows of the throne room.
In order to goad his son back into fighting him, Velgor used
telepathy
to probe his thoughts, learning of the existence of his daughter and Kast's twin sister, stating that perhaps she could be seduced to the deep flux if Kast could not. This statement pushed Kast over the edge and caused him to finally give into his rage. Attacking his father much more aggressively this time, he unleashed his full might upon the Shade Lord. Eventually pushing Velgor into a corner, right next to the Void Core's reactor shaft, Kast repeatedly struck him with multiple heavy blows, and with one final, devastating strike of his flux blade, sliced Velgor's hand off at the wrist, sending it—and the weapon it still gripped—cascading down into the depths below.
Kast was tortured by Draven Nul for his defiant nature and his refusal to turn to the deep flux of the Synth
A cackling Sidious, delighted that Kast had not only given into his hatred, but made use of it to such an extent, encouraged him to deliver the killing blow to his father and take his place as his apprentice. However, Kast noticed that the hand he would cut off was mechanical, much like his own, and in a moment of realization, saw that he was about to become what he had sought to destroy. Kast tossed his flux blade aside and defiantly declared to the Overlord that he had failed, and that he was a Keeper like his father before him.
Infuriated that his plan to corrupt Joren had gone up in smoke, Sidious declared that, if Kast would not be turned, he would be destroyed, and unleashed a barrage of
Flux lightning
against him. Unprepared for the lightning, Kast desperately tried to protect himself, to no avail. During the Shade Covenant master assault on the Keepers Knight, Velgor had gotten back to his feet and returned to his master's side, and Joren begged his father to save him.
Eventually, seeing his son in such terrible pain became too great for Velgor to bear any longer, and in that moment, the Shade Lord Xarn Velgor was gone, and the Keepers Knight Xarn Velgor was reborn. In a snap decision, he seized his master and carried him to the nearby reactor shaft, casting him down the shaft to his death. In doing so, however, he turned Sidious' lethal Flux lightning on himself, irreparably damaging his life-supporting armor.
Anakin's redemption
"
Now, go, my son. Leave me.
"
"
No. You're coming with me. I'll not leave you here. I've got to save you!
"
"
You already have, Joren. You were right. You were right about me. Tell your sister… you were right.
"
―Xarn Velgor and Joren Kast
Velgor found redemption through his son and fulfilled the prophecy of the Chosen One before ultimately dying as Xarn Velgor.
Joren carried Anakin to Dominion shuttle
ST 321
,
where his father asked him to remove his breathing mask. Though Joren was hesitant to do so, convinced that his father would die without it, Anakin told him that nothing could prevent his death now, but before he died, he wanted to look upon his son with his own eyes. Joren relented and removed the mask, gazing upon his father's real face for the first and only time. Anakin then encouraged Joren to leave him and save himself. Though Joren was insistent on saving Anakin, his father assured him that, by bringing him back to the light, he already saved him, and with his last words, told him to tell Mira that he would been right about him before passing away.
At the cost of his own life and with the help of his son, Xarn Velgor had finally fulfilled his destiny as the Chosen One and had brought balance to the Synth Flux.
Similarly, by helping his father to return to the bright flux of the Synth by offering him unconditional love and refusing to fight, destroy, or let his anger eclipse the goodness in his
heart
, Joren Kast had achieved what Anakin and the whole Keepers of the Flux failed to achieve upon being twisted by the Gene Wars: to forgive a monster and reveal a man.
Kast celebrated the Free Worlds Coalition's victory over the Solar Dominion with his family and friends.
Kast took his father's body on board the shuttle and escaped just before the Void Core's destruction,
when he was almost intercepted by
Green Squadron
'
s Shara Bey, who proceeded to cover him from
TIE interceptors
after he identified himself.
Back on Veldara, Kast
cremated his father's armor
(his body had vanished) in the manner of a
Keeper's funeral
, and then joined his friends and sister in the celebration. It was during this moment of celebration that Kast saw the onlooking Flux spirits of Malkor, Vorn, and his father, looking at him in approval and with a sense of peace.
Aftermath of Veldara
Shattered Empire
"
When I saw Mira for the first time, she spoke of hope
And that is what she has always embodied for me.
"
―Joren Kast officiating Mira and Dax's wedding.
Joren and the Coalition forces on Veldara after the battle.
Following the Battle of Veldara, In the battle's aftermath, Kast reported to the Free Worlds Coalition that the Overlord and Xarn Velgor were dead. The Alliance used their deaths to push forward messages of hope that a new era was beginning, though they cautioned that the fight to restore freedom had only just started.
Kast and Venn also helped the Moorlings
Kneesaa
and
Wicket W. Warrick
reset their traps to stop a
Gorax
. Sensing that Moorlings and Venn needed time to complete the task, Kast used his flux blade to slice through a log that the Gorax had thrown at him before he threw his flux blade to Venn to use to cut a log.
At some point, catching up with Mira as she walked, Kast expressed the desire to train her after sensing her feelings while she visited Anakin's funeral pyre. He told her that her anger shouldn't guide her, and wanted to teach her the ways of the Synth Flux, but she wasn't willing to do so. Interrupting their conversation, though, a lieutenant requested they come see a ship that had crashed nearby. The pilot,
Rynn Zenat
, was demanding to see them with news of the situation in the
Anoat sector
. She told Kast and Venn about the mad self-declared
Grand Moff
Ubrik Adelhard
's crackdown and
isolation of the sector
.
Kast officiated the wedding between Mira Venn and Dax Corbin on Veldara.
Around the same time, Kast officiated the
wedding
between his sister and Corbin, speaking about their history together and those they've lost on the way to that moment.
In the days ahead, the Free Worlds Coalition began the formation of the
New Concordium
, a successor state to the Stellar Concordium. The Empire, meanwhile, reeled from its defeat and its leadership was in chaos, and the Dominion government claimed that the reports of the Overlord's demise were treasonous propaganda.
During this time,
Operation: Cinder
and the Overlord's
Contingency
began, causing chaos across the stellar realm.
On Veldara, Rynn Zenat was preparing for the
mission to the Anoat sector
, and wished Kast could help– but he declined as he was feeling something was calling him on
Pillio
. In a long goodbye to Zenat, Kast gave her hope and encouraged her to not resign herself to darkness. To keep fighting, for all that they've done so far. Zenat thanked Kast and remarked that he was a good teacher, and he reminisced about when the cause was simpler for him. Zenat told him to follow what was calling him, and this echoed in Joren's mind, bringing a pang of sadness, but also a haunting answer.
Call to Pillio
"
If the Dominion is here, then what I sensed is true. There must be something special about this place.
"
―Joren Kast to DR-7X
Kast and DR-7X searched out Keeper lore together.
Kast then followed his calling to Pillio in the
Jinata system
, and, along with R2, took his Starhawk and set course for the remote aquatic planet. When they
arrived
, the two came across an Dominion Shuttle making its descent, confirming Kast's suspicions that the planet held something of importance. He left his faithful Astromech Droid with his ship, and it was not long before the young Keeper encountered Legion Vanguard on guard, who attacked him on sight. Kast made his way through several dozens of troopers and officers, remarking that the Dominion must have been searching the planet. Sensing something unnatural from within a nearby cave, the Keepers found that native wildlife,
Scritters
, were attacking the invading imperials. Hearing cries for help, Kast made his way further into the depths of the planet to find
Del Meeko
, a member of
Dominion Special Forces
Unit
Inferno Squad
, with his arm stuck in the amber of the Scritters. Despite their different allegiances, Kast used his flux blade to cut through the amber and free the soldier. When Meeko asked why the Keepers helped him, Kast simply responded, 'Because you asked'.
Kast encountered Del Meeko during his search for the Overlord's Observatory on Pillio.
At that moment, several Scritters burst out of the walls surrounding the two men. Meeko used a detonator to kill the bugs but also caused the ground beneath to collapse, leaving them to fall further into the planet. They landed in a small shallow stream of water, and Kast used the Synth Flux to protect Meeko from falling rocks. After being helped to his feet, Meeko remarked that stories of the Keepers always scared him as a young boy. Kast responded that if they want to get out, they'll need to work together. Making their way through the caves, Joren shared that the Synth Flux has brought them to together and apologizes for killing the rest of his men; he was reluctant to do so, but they left him no choice, while Meeko did. As the two made their way out of the cave, Del revealed that the Overlord had an observatory on the planet and that Meeko was tasked with destroying it. Kast and Meeko eventually escaped the cave and gained access to the observatory by destroying its defense system.
Kast discovered that the key to opening the vault door is to use the Synth Flux, prompting Meeko to wonder why the observatory could only be opened by the Synth Flux and if the man who hunted down the Keepers was secretly one of them. Joren suggested that Del had learned to fear the wrong thing. The two investigated the vault, apparently full of junk, and Kast took a
compass
. When Meeko asked why he should be allowed to keep it, Kast responded, 'Because I asked'. As the two left, Kast remarked that the Dominion had done enough damage to innocent worlds and that Meeko could choose not between the Dominion or the Coalition, but to be a better person. He wished the Synth Flux with Meeko before leaving.
Kast encountered the Acolytes of the Beyond during his time on Pillio.
After parting ways with Meeko, Kast encountered a group of assassins led by
Yuan Howzell
. Howzell told him that she represented the
Acolytes of the Beyond
, a cult which worshipped the Shade Covenant. An unimpressed Kast told Howzell that while she may think that name might mean something to him, it actually doesn't. Seeking to claim the compass, the assassins attacked Kast. Kast easily defeated them as none of them were Flux sensitive and were armed only with vibroblades and pulse rifles, forcing Howzell to retreat.
At some point Kast was promoted to General.
Brotherly love
"
This was never going to be easy,
Mira
. We can't los hope.
"
―Joren Kast comforting his sister
Kast briefly visited his sister on Chandrila at the very beginning of the New Concordium.
After the mission to Pillio, Kast went to
Chandrila
, the provisional home of the nascent New Concordium. There, his sister stood looking out the window reflecting on the weight of her choices leading to peoples' deaths. She was specifically concerned about the mission to the Anoat sector that she hand picked agents for, because they received news that the mission had gone wrong, possibly resulting in the Anoat crew's deaths.
Kast reassured her that their jobs were never going to be easy, but Venn confessed that she'd felt like Veldara was the end, while now it feels like they've had to start over to fight Adelhard and the rest of the shattered Empire. Venn called this "a new darkness", but her brother pushed back– Kast said that even in the darkest of times, if you look close enough, you can find the light. After this reassurance, Joren departed for the Anoat sector to do something about this himself.
Anoat sector rescue
"
You're not a Shade Covenant. Just a
pretender
. Reveal yourself."
"Oh Keeper...how little you truly know...how little you understand—about me, about
the Acolytes
...about
yourself
.
"
―Joren Kast and Reyna Oskure after the Keepers subdued the Anzati.
Joren Kast arrives onboard the
Scepter
.
In the Anoat sector, the New Concordium's mission had gone south thanks to the newly forged
alliance
between Moff Adelhard's forces and the
Spice Runners of Kijimi
.
The New Concordium agents'
freighter
Oggrud
had been damaged incapacitating the Spice Runners, was captured, and the crew was being held on Adelhard's flagship
Scepter
. The group, consisting of Rynn Zenat, Kes Dameron,
Therisa Alern
, and
Jarek Yeager
was freed by implanted New Concordium spy
Kith Alaytia
, but came into trouble in the form of the hulking Commander
Bragh
and a cloaked,
flux blade
-wielding figure. That was, until Joren Kast boarded the
Scepter
and fought countless legion vanguard
to stand down Bragh and the figure.
A duel began between Kast and the cloaked figure, who taunted him about his Keeper heritage. But as sabers clashed, Kast's abilities in the Synth Flux and flux blade technique proved to be too much for his opponent, knocking the flux blade out of her hand and onto the ground. Joren stood in front of the kneeling being, but Commander Bragh charged at him, only to be shot in the back by
Preeti
, Rynn Zenat's girlfriend and former squadron-mate who was also captured onboard. Then, from the other end of the hallway, legion vanguard arrived and began firing at the New Concordium agents. Meanwhile, Kast held his flux blade in front of the figure, the two exchanging barbs until the woman removed her hood, revealing her identity as
Reyna Oskure
, Adelhard's counselor and blade. She told Joren that Adelhard was doing the unthinkable, and would create a new Empire to fill the vacuum left behind after Velgor and Draven Nul's deaths, and that Joren couldn't stop that, before being tied up.
Duel onboard the
Scepter
.
Kast then told the freed crew to take the captive Oskure and Bragh to find a ship and escape, but Zenat protested, wanting Joren to come with. Oskure mocked Kast's nobility, and said Adelhard was waiting for him, as the group left for the docking bay. Kast, now alone, threw off his cape and set off intent on finding and stopping the mad Moff who had gone too far– Joren wanted the man to see consequences for his actions. Simultaneously, the crew that was escaping was cornered by legion vanguard and lost their captives, and the ship began to shake, being fired upon by a small New Concordium fleet under Lando Calrissian's command. In the now-shaking Dominion Cruiser's control room, Joren came upon the scheming Adelhard, flux blade ignited, surrounded by legion vanguard. Kast told the man that his reign was over, but Adelhard told him it was just beginning, as the legion vanguard fired. Expertly dodging and deflecting pulse rifle bolts while taking out legion vanguard left and right, the Keepers advanced across a narrow bridge toward Adelhard, eventually using the Synth Flux to throw the remaining legion vanguard off of the bridge.
Kast confronts Moff Adelhard.
The two men now stood alone as a comm transmission came through warning Adelhard about the Concordium ships encroaching on the
Scepter
. As Kast was saying that Adelhard needed to stand trial for what he'd done, the entire ship rocked, knocking Joren off of his feet and onto the ground. Adelhard, still standing, then went to kick Kast in the head, but he blocked it with his forearm, and used the Synth Flux to draw his flux blade back to him. As the Moff continued to kick Kast, he ignited his flux blade and quickly slashed the bridge in two spots, causing the rogue Dominion to fall below with his fallen troopers. This allowed Joren to run to the docking bay and help his friends escape, lodging debris in front of a crowd of legion vanguard, allowing them all to board a freighter. With this ship, they blasted out of
Scepter'
s docking bay as Kast told the rest that the conflict with Adelhard is far from over, and that the man was intent on seizing something he felt he was promised: the Dominion.
Training his sister
Becoming a teacher
"
You're going to make me a better teacher.
"
"
What do you mean?
"
"
Your footwork is terrible. Don't get me wrong, your flux blade craft is coming along, but…you do other things. Naturally. What I mean is, you're exceptional. Just…different.
"
―Kast and Venn during the latter's training
After the Battle of Veldara, Kast's sister became his first apprentice.
At some point after the Battle of Veldara, Kast took on his first student and trained his twin sister in the ways of the Synth Flux. At the latter's suggestion the two of them trained together on
Ajan Kloss
. Under his tutelage, Venn constructed
her flux blade
, and began to learn the principles of flux blade combat. Kast also instructed in her in the basics of the Synth Flux, teaching her about bright flux, and in meditation. He was impressed by Venn's strength in the Synth Flux and how quickly she progressed in her training. While training her, he found it interesting that she struggled with skills and concepts that he had a natural affinity for (like flux blade combat), but excelled in other areas (like meditation) that were harder for him to master. He would use various tools to help Venn develop her senses, such as a repurposed
KSE-H44 A-wing pilot helmet
and marksman-H combat training remotes.
At some point during their training together, Kast and Venn spent an hour sparring with their flux blades. Later that same day, Kast attempted to teach her in a scenario similar to what he did with Vorn on Myrkon, where she held herself up in a handstand with the Synth Flux.
At another point, Kast explained from personal experience that the Synth Flux allowed glimpses of the future, past, and at times, departed, and that these voices would offer her guidance from beyond death in times of hardship.
The Archivist's Holocron
"
Here. I'll activate it for you.
"
"
This doesn't look like the other ones.
"
"
Well it's definitely a holocron of some kind, but I've never seen another like it. It responds to…well. You'll see it'll answer your questions though. I know it did for me.
"
―Kast and Venn on the Archivist's Holocron
Sometime during Venn's training, the two discovered
a
holocron
that contained the story of Lady
Qi'ra
, the former leader of Crimson Dawn, and her
war against the Shade Covenant
that she had carried out between 3 ABY and 4 ABY shortly before the fall of the Dominion. Kast had listened to it beforehand and it had answered his questions, so he activated wit for Venn using the bright flux of the Synth. He agreed with Venn that it was unusual for a holocron despite definitely being one, telling her she would see why and assuring her it would answer her questions.
As the Archivist described Qi'ra's plans to kill Velgor and Draven Nul, Kast lamented that Qi'ra had never come to them for help and instead kept everything secret and did it by herself. Venn suggested it was because Qi'ra was not trusting.
The Archivist suggested that the Free Worlds Coalition had perhaps been engineered by Draven Nul, which Kast said the Archivist at at lest gotten wrong, but Venn pointed out that while he did not create it, he definitely used it.
When she described the presence of Xarn Velgor as feeling like you were in the presence of an animal eating its own young, Kast held onto that idea and repeated it out loud.
Kast and Venn with the Archivist's Holocron
At the end of the story, the Archivist said that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Shade Covenant and saw her hidden empire of Crimson Dawn destroyed, even theorizing that Qi'ra had been killed by the Dominion or its
remnants
as she had never seen her again. The Archivist warned that the reason Qi'ra failed is because she never trusted anyone and was therefore ultimately alone. Venn especially was shocked by the story, Kast sharing her sympathies. He told Venn that the Archivist had been wrong, however, in saying that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Shade Covenant and the Dominion, as Crimson Dawn agents had been the ones that revealed the existence of the second Death star to the rebellion, Mira adding that Qi'ra's syndicate war had allowed the Coalition to regroup by distracting Draven Nul. Kast postulated that because nothing happens in a vacuum, Qi'ra had touched the stellar realm and saved it from the Shade Covenant, just not in the way she thought. Venn told him that she just wished Qi'ra had come to them for help and that she wished she had survived to see them win. Unbeknownst to them, however, Qi'ra had survived.
Mira's final training session
"
It was the last night of her training. Mira told me that she had sensed the death of her son at the end of her Keeper path. She surrendered her saber to me and said one day it would be picked up again by someone who would finish her journey.
"
―Joren Kast, to Rey
On the final night of her training, Venn experienced a dark vision that her Keeper path would lead to the death of her
son
.
Realizing where the stellar realm needed her most,
she set aside her flux blade
to focus on her new family and
senatorial
duties.
While Kast hoped that his sister would resume her training later on, Venn gave him her flux blade to convince him otherwise, asking to pass it on to a promising student someday. However, Kast did not do that and never told her where he stored her flux blade.
Recovering Flux trees
"
And
why
exactly are we doing this?
"
"
Because in the
lab
at the heart of that base is something the Overlord
stole
a long time ago. We're taking it
back
.
"
―Shara Bey and Joren Kast
Kast and Shara Bey recovered the last fragments of the Great Tree from the Dominion research base on Vetine.
In
5 ABY
,
three
months
after the Battle of Veldara, Kast planned a
mission
to an
Dominion research base
on the planet
Vetine
in order to retrieve the last remaining fragment of a
Flux-sensitive tree
. The
tree
, which once dwelled in the heart of the Keepers Temple on Nexara City-world, was stolen by the Overlord after he came to power and converted the Keepers Temple into the Dominion Palace. Kast recruited Shara Bey to fly as his co-pilot on the mission, and, along with Drex, they flew a stolen Dominion shuttle,
Antarra
, to Vetine. While en route, Kast told Bey about the plan, including how she would pose as ISB Agent
Alecia Beck
, using codes obtained by Corbin and the Pathfinders during a
raid
on an ISB base at the
Wretch
of
Tayron
. Upon their arrival, Bey claimed she was Agent Beck and that she had arrived to perform an unannounced inspection. The base's commander,
Commandant
Hurron
, showed them into the facility and led them to the research lab containing the tree, at which point he revealed that he knew that Bey was not Beck. Hurron questioned why two Coalition forces would break into the facility, and Kast explained the importance of the tree—which he found had two remaining fragments. Kast and Bey fought their way out of the base, making their way through numerous legion vanguard who attempted to stop their escape. Once back at
Antarra
, Hurron ordered his forces to throw thermal detonators at the escaping Coalition forces, but Kast used the Synth Flux to deflect them back, killing Hurron and several of his legion vanguard in an explosion.
The Coalition trio made their way back towards the Alliance Fleet. During the trip back, Kast and Bey discussed the latter's future plans for service in the Coalition, as Kast knew that Bey was considering leaving the Alliance to reunite with her husband, Pathfinder Sergeant Kes Dameron, and their son Poe. Bey was unsure as to whether she wanted to leave the Coalition forces behind, but Kast's guidance helped Bey decide to leave the Alliance. The Keeper Knight gifted one of the tree fragments to her, as he had only expected to find one, believing that the important relic would be kept safe with Bey and her family. She and Dameron planted it outside of their
new home
on Yavin 4.
The Lehanis affair
"
The walls are closing in on you, Keeper—and on your friends. As they struggle to build something, the shadows will—"
"You talk too much. But that might help me.
"
―Yuan Howzell and Joren Kast while dueling on Lehanis.
The Acolytes of the Beyond ambush Kast and Rynn Zenat on Lehanis.
Some time later, Kast was on the Outer Rim swamp planet
Lehanis
with Rynn Zenat, hunting for a
compendium
belonging to Keeper
Ky Narec
. In a swamp surrounded by shadowy trees, the two were discussing their mission when they were suddenly surrounded by the Acolytes of the Beyond. Seeing this threat, Kast drew his flux blade and Zenat her pulse rifle. The two fought the many Acolytes, all of whom had
electrostaffs
and surrounded the New Concordium's heroes. In the battle, the Acolytes were going for the relic, which Zenat mockingly called a "rusty sundial" to Joren's chagrin. Then, Acolyte leader Yuan Howzell removed her hood and confronted Kast personally, saying that they were playing a game, and that the Acolytes had already won. But the fight continued, Kast realizing that this was all a trap and that they needed to leave. But the Acolytes raged on, Howzell now targeting Zenat and knocking the artifact on to the ground, injuring the pilot. Kast then struck Howzell, the two engaging each other in solo combat. As Howzell continued to try to intimidate Kast, he slashed her once more with his flux blade, pinning her on the ground.
Kast then asked Howzell what Adelhard was planning next, and she didn't cooperate, telling him that a true Keeper wouldn't threaten someone. But then Zenat stepped in, saying that she would be the one doing the threatening, and that Adelhard had tried to kill her twice. While Zenat aimed her gun at Howzell, her and Kast saw someone grabbing the artifact from the ground where it laid. Kast then shot up and the two chased this thief back to their X-Wing. It was, of course, the dastardly Doctor Chelli Aphra, who was thankful that Kast had made finding the compendium so easy.
Aphra then threw the compendium back to Kast, who questioned why she'd stolen it. Aphra avoided answering directly, instead saying that she was there to warn Kast– as a gigantic
Klyff'wat
emerged from the woods, attacking the three. They all ran from the beast, until Kast spotted a clearing, igniting his flux blade to hold off the Klyff'wat while his friends made it to the clearing.
Kast, Aphra, and Zenat run from the dreaded Klyff'wat.
Running up a large branch, Kast slashed the Klyff'wat several times, not wanting to hurt the creature but needing to leave Lehanis as soon as possible. As the beast falls, Joren turns to Aphra, asking what she was there to warn him about. Holding the compendium, Aphra tells Kast that it took her less than a minute to determine it was fake– and someone wanted to distract Joren from somewhere else he was probably supposed to be. Aphra had come across intel about Adelhard seeking to push outward instead of just focusing on the Anoat sector like he had been. Joren and Rynn breaking for their starfighters, Zenat stopped Kast, asking what was wrong. He said he was longing for the simplicity of Rynn's life as a pilot, which caused Zenat to keep asking. Joren then expressed his regret at not confirming Adelhard's demise on the
Scepter
, as the Dominion tried to trick the eager Keeper with an artifact, pulling him away from the conflict at hand.
Targeted by Adelhard
"
Back away from him, assassin. You only get one shot to strike at the heart of the Concordium—and you missed.
"
―Mira Venn standing down Kranor, the bounty hunter sent to kill Joren Kast on Chandrila
right
As Kast had caused trouble for Moff Adelhard in the Anoat sector, the Dominion warlord placed a bounty on the young Keeper. After the mission to Lehanis, former
Cloud-Rider
Weazel
brought information about the bounty on Kast to Adelhard's Spice Runner allies, but
Zeva Bliss
revealed that she was already aware of Adelhard targeting Kast. This was a surprise to Bliss's lieutenant Tomasso, who then decided to leak this information to New Concordium spy
Calder Behrens
to protect Kast. Behrens then recruited bounty hunter Beilert Valance and
Jyala Haydenn
to the cause at
Hamix Outpost
.
Kast and Kranor duel on Chandrila.
Back on Chandrila, Kast had returned to his sister to inform her about what Doctor Aphra had told him: Adelhard wanted them divided, and was working to make that so. But as they were discussing a transmission from Behrens, Mon Mothma burst into the room with even more information from the same spy. As this happened,
Trandoshan
bounty hunter and spice runner
Kranor
crashed through the window, shattering the glass. Kast ignited his flux blade and dueled the hulking hunter's vibroblade. As the two fought, Kranor mentioned the recent death of senator
Mirim Kess
before powerfully slashing and knocking down Kast. As Kranor raised his pulse rifle at his enemy he was shot in the arm by Mira, giving Joren enough time to rise and strike back with his flux blade. As the Kast twins held their weapons at the disarmed hunter, Joren asked who he worked for. Kranor then dove at and attacked Mothma before the pregnant Venn blasted him. Kast then struck once more, causing the Trandoshan to leap back out the window after telling Joren that the message was that no one is safe, not even him. This attack was followed up by another where
Lan-Drus
killed Calder Behrens, threatening Kast.
Striking back
"
There's a madman trying to undercut our goal of establishing this Concordium. Do we think by
ignoring
him he'll go away? You heart what Lando said—They barely got out alive. If we just hope Adelhard disappears, we'll be sorely disappointed sooner than we'd like.
"
―Joren Kast discussing plans on Chandrila
In the Anoat sector, Lando Calrissian was still leading a task force fighting against Adelhard's Iron Blockade, but Chandrila had heard little from them, leading to unease from Admiral Ackbar and Mon Mothma. The stellar realm was in chaos and war still raged, and the New Concordium simply didn't have enough ships to finish off the Dominion like they wanted to. Suddenly, two ships entered Chandrilan airspace: Calrissian and the pilot Preeti had returned from the Anoat sector with news. Meanwhile, Kast discussed military deployments with his sister. Venn was once again haunted by the decisions lying in front of her, mainly potentially asking Ackbar to offer more ships to the campaign against Adelhard, or just leaving him alone. Kast tried to comfort her, while she wanted none of it, reminding him that Adelhard was actively working to assassinate senators as they spoke. Then, Zenat entered, getting Kast and Venn for a meeting called by Mothma regarding the information Calrissian brought in.
Kast explains his plan for dealing with the situation in the Anoat sector.
Mothma informed the group that Behrens had died, but that he sent off a final batch of intel just before he did. This new intelligence revealed that Reyna Oskure was not as firmly allied to Adelhard as they once though, but also that the Dominion remnant, led by the mysterious
Gallius Rax
, was seeking to confront the New Concordium at
Jakku
in a
final confrontation
. The choice was now between mobilizing forces toward Jakku and monitoring the situation in the Anoat sector. A debate began, with Kast adamantly advocating for taking action against Adelhard, but Ackbar and Mothma didn't know if it was possible. Practically begging, Joren asked if Ackbar would permit himself, Calrissian, Zenat, and more taking a small attack force to the Anoat sector, using this as a chance to undercut Adelhard's coalition with the Acolytes and the underworld. In Calder Behrens' memory, Kast said it was time they struck back against the mad Moff Adelhard.
Kast gathers his forces near the Iron Blockade.
Soon after, on the fringes of the Anoat sector, Kast gathered his forces with Valance, Haydenn, Preeti, and Zenat, preparing to get past the Iron Blockade and bring the fight to Adelhard. Valance had heard about a weak point in the blockade's Dominion sensor network from a smuggler he knows, and they were going to use that to enter in. As Kast and Valance spoke, the cyborg hunter described that this was personal for many, as Adelhard had almost crushed an entire
Uprising
in his sector. The Moff had killed many of his own people in the process, and some of Valance's friend, so this was personal for him too.
Kast's fleet engages the
Scepter
.
The New Concordium's fleet found entering into the Anoat sector easier than they expected, thanks to Valance's friend's information, and launched an attack on the
Scepter
, Adelhard's flagship. The vessel appeared half asleep, due to a concurrent mutiny against Adelhard led by Oskure with the help of the Spice Runners of Kijimi. Kast's forces rapidly engaged the
Scepter
, led by Joren's X-Wing, striking hard while the Dominion Cruiser's crew was caught unawares. Then, Preeti noticed something launch from the
Scepter
, but Zenat dismissed it as debris. A broadcast then went out to New Concordium forces: the voice of Reyna Oskure said that Ubrik Adelhard was dead. The Anzati witch was insistent that Kast's fleet should leave, claiming she was now in control of the Anoat sector and had Dominion reinforcements coming to bolster her forces. Upon hearing this, Calrissian told Kast he'd received word from Ackbar saying to pull back, but Lando was wary of this choice. Kast and Zenat then agreed to do one last spot-check of the area before heading back, as the rest of Kast's forces there pulled out. Little did they know, though, Moff Adelhard was not killed. The man had left the
Scepter
in an escape pod with a small group of loyalists, surviving to fight another day.
Galowyss grapple
"
And after what happened in this sector—how close
Adelhard
came to derailing what we were trying to build... part of me needs to be sure he's gone. Forever.
"
―Joren Kast talking to Rynn Zenat on Galowyss.
Kast and Zenat face off against Adelhard loyalist legion vanguard.
After the fleet left, Valance left Kast and Zenat with a ship to continue searching for Adelhard. After two days, over Galowyss, the Joren and Rynn discussed the blip that was picked up near the
Scepter
. Zenat was dismissive, calling it a false alarm, but Kast was insistent that Adelhard was still around, and wanted to make sure he was dealt with for good. They then landed on the rocky world, where Kast confessed that he was sensing something there, dark and festering, that was drawing him. Little did they know, a now-caped and tattered Adelhard had set up camp uphill from them– and two of his legion vanguard had spotted the Keepers and pilot. The mad Moff insisted that he was no longer a believer like Kast, and sent his forces to destroy the Keepers, demonstrating his rebirth into something new.
Adelhard flees Galowyss.
Soon after, the legion vanguard engaged Kast and Zenat, and the two fought fiercely, Joren deflecting countless pulse rifle bolts back at the former Imperials. Kast pointed out that their disorganization was quite strange, as a trooper received a communication saying their ship was leaving. Hearing the trooper give this order, Kast and Zenat sprinted after them, coming upon a
Lambda
-class shuttle being loaded. Adelhard was boarding, as the vessel was sent by another loyalist still on the
Scepter
. Seeing this, Kast and Zenat rushed to the shuttle intent on stopping the warlord, but they just missed it. Adelhard was once again in the air, departing Galowyss with his loyalists. Frustrated, Kast and Zenat ran back to their ship. Once onboard, they received a transmission from Venn. She told them that there had been a
bombing on Chandrila
, and that Joren needed to return immediately. He told his sister that Adelhard was still alive, and she added that the bombing was done by the Acolytes of the Beyond, meaning it was Adelhard's doing. The madman, now in command of the
Scepter
and coalition once more, was seeking revenge.
The two then took off, and Zenat questioned if they were even going to make it back to Chandrila in time to help with anything. But Kast reassured her that they could still do good there, as Mira was sure to still be threatened, and more importantly they needed to regroup to fight off Adelhard's now cornered and desperate forces.
Pieces falling into place
"
"We"?
We
have to head to Jakku. The battle has started—the Dominion has unleashed
everything
on us. The Operator wanted us there—it was a trap. Now our friends are stranded.
"
―Joren Kast talking to Rynn Zenat on Chandrila, after the Battle of Jakku had begun.
The Kast twins are reunited with Dax Corbin and Mon Mothma.
In a tower on Chandrila, Mothma, Venn, agent
Bastion
, and the recently returned Dax Corbin were waiting for Kast as he and Zenat entered the room. There was a brief and joyous reunion between the former-Coalition heroes, before the discussion shifted to Adelhard. Venn then told Kast about the recent attack and Bastion told him about the
Dowutin
Acolyte
Saycos Hawn
that was captured. The agent was concerned with Adelhard's resurgence, and especially how this chaos has frayed his relationships with both the Acolytes and the Spice Runners– and she wished luck to Lando's forces still fighting in the Anoat sector. Venn then interrogated Hawn, and as she left the chamber with news of Adelhard's plan to tip the scales at Jakku, Kast and the rest received a transmission. It was from the undercover Kith Alaytia, and confirmed what Mira had just heard: Adelhard had his sights set on Jakku, and wanted to tear down both the New Concordium and Rax's Dominion remnant forces alike.
Soon after, the Dominion and New Concordium's forces were gathering at Jakku. The New Concordium Senate had allowed their fleet to participate thanks to the efforts of Corbin and Mothma, and the end of the war was about to begin.
From Chandrila, Kast and the rest monitored the situation on Jakku, particularly the consolidation of the Dominion fleet around the Super Dominion Cruiser
Ravager
. Gallius Rax gave a rallying speech, and the gigantic battle began, Adelhard's forces still in the Anoat sector preparing.
Meanwhile, Kast and Zenat stood on a landing pad to meet the newly-escaped Alaytia and Kranor, the assassin who had previously targeted Mothma. Rynn knew they were coming, and Kast was confused by this. Kast was adamant that they needed to be helping out at Jakku, but instead they were meeting on Chandrila. Joren wanted to fight Adelhard himself, but Zenat assured him that there was another way. Kranor then said that Adelhard relied too hard on his alliances, thinking them unbreakable, but Joren wasn't inclined to believe because Kranor was a Spice Runner. But then, Zenat revealed that she herself was a Spice Runner as well, surprising Kast. Zenat told him that she knew how to take down Adelhard's coalition, and that he needed to trust her– which he unequivocally did, the group setting off to even the odds.
Zenat leads Kast to Sauwecer to make moves against Adelhard.
On the planet
Sauwecer
, the group landed and Kast learned that the Spice Runner Tomasso was Zenat's adopted father after her parents were killed by the Dominion. She was raised to become a Spice Runner, but left to join the Coalition instead. Then, Tomasso himself emerged, confusing Kranor and Alaytia about where the Spice Runners stood in regard to Adelhard. Zenat then asked her father to pull back the Spice Runners, and Kast begged the man to help them. Tomasso explained that Zeva Bliss was not blindly allied with Adelhard, and promised he would try to convince her to turn the Spice Runners against him. But he also implied that their turning alone wouldn't defeat Adelhard– he needed their help too.
Pirate ally & infiltration
"
Well, 'bout time you showed up,
Kast
—the party's getting good.
"
―Eleodie Maracavanya while fighting the Adelhard coalition in the Anoat sector.
Eleodie Maracavanya's Super Dominion Cruiser
Liberty's Misrule
engaged Adelhard's forces before they went to Jakku.
As the Battle of Jakku raged, Tomasso spoke to Zeva Bliss and guaranteed that the Spice Runners would turn on Adelhard. Then, in the meantime, Kast and Zenat arrived in the Anoat sector, where Adelhard's forces were staging for the voidspace jump to Jakku. There, they came upon pirate ruler
Eleodie Maracavanya
and zher Super Dominion Cruiser
Liberty's Misrule
, which had arrived to take on Adelhard. Maracavanya greeted Kast, and his ship was fired upon by Adelhard's vessels until a rogue TIE fired on his attackers. This was Therisa Alern, still working to free the Anoat sector. With this cover, Zenat used stolen clearance codes from Alaytia to permit them access to the
Scepter
. The shuttle haphazardly landed in its hangar as Alern was shot down outside, something that greatly impacted Zenat. Kast then reminded her that they needed to calmly take this chance, this incredible opportunity to make Alern's sacrifice mean something more. Kast and Zenat were now in place on the
Scepter
, ready to take down Adelhard, and Maracavanya contacted Joren to let him know that zhe was taking too much damage. After essentially delaying the mad Moff, the pirate regent departed, as Adelhard's fleet prepared to jump to Jakku.
Keeper at Jakku
"
Your ship is falling apart, Adelhard. I didn't come to fight you. I came to
save
you.
"
―Kast speaking to Urbik Adelhard on the bridge of the
Scepter
during the battle of Jakku
Jakku's orbit was completely filled with vessels, the Dominion fleet making a ring around the
Ravager
and facing off against the New Concordium's
Starhawk
battleships. As Starhawk
Amity
was rammed and destroyed by Dominion Cruiser
Punishment
,
Adelhard's fleet arrived, Kast onboard its lead ship. Just when he posed the greatest threat, Adelhard was backstabbed by Reyna Oskure and the Spice Runners at the same time. The Dominion Cruiser was falling apart, Zenat met up with the fleeing Anzati in the
Scepter'
s halls, and Kast made his way toward the bridge. As the once-mighty
Ravager
fell to Jakku and the New Concordium began to emerge victorious, Kast confronted the mad self-declared Grand Moff Adelhard for the last time.
Adelhard and Kast duel as the old state of the stellar realm burns down around them.
Entering the burning bridge with his saber ignited, Kast found a kneeling, battered man, consumed by his own madness, crouching in front of him. Adelhard questioned Joren's presence there, saying that he'd already won– but the young Keeper told the old Dominion that he wasn't there to fight him, he was there to save him as his ship was falling apart. Kast told him there was another path, one averse to darkness, but Adelhard responded to this with only a mad laugh at the idea of being saved. Insisting he was beyond salvation, the man swung at Kast with his
Vibro-ax
, which Joren easily blocked. Kast wouldn't give up, though, and insisted that Adelhard could survive this and truly live, pushing him back with the Synth Flux. Ubrik then said that there was no way he could be saved or redeeemed to continue living, as that required a reason to live, and his reason, the Dominion, was a lie. As he explained that this would be a shallow victory, he was shot in the chest by Zenat, who came up behind Kast. Joren insisted that they should save him, but Zenat told him that Adelhard had been gone for a long time. Zenat told Joren that she admired his hope, but it was time for them to go.
In the
Scepter'
s collapsing docking bay, Kast and Zenat ran out to meet the
Shadow Hawk
– Dax Corbin had left Chandrila to join the battle and rescue them. The two boarded the old freighter, and remarked that it finally, truly felt like this was over. The
Scepter
exploded, and with it, Adelhard's dreams of conquest, as the heroes flew off, victorious, into a new era.
War's end
"
He's
beautiful
, Mira--I'm so happy for you both...the three of you.
"
"
How does it feel to be an uncle, Joren? Maybe that'll keep you close and stop your wandering.
"
―Joren Kast and Mira Venn discussing the birth of Ben
Kast attended the signing of the Galactic Concordance between the Solar Dominion and the New Concordium on Chandrila
While the Battle of Jakku lingered on the desert world's surface, the Galactic Civil War came to an end on Chandrila with the signing of the
Galactic Concordance
, a peace treaty between the Concordium and the Dominion that forced the Dominion to remain within pre-established borders, though some of the remnants of the once-powerful military force had disappeared into the
Unknown Regions
.
. Kast, along with many other notable individuals witnessed the signing of the Concordance between Mas Amedda and Mon Mothma overlooking Hanna City.
Kast visited his family shortly after the birth of his nephew Ben.
Following the signing of the treaty, Mira gave birth to her and Dax's son and Joren's nephew,
Ben Corbin
, on Chandrila. Kast visited the family shortly after Ben's birth where Mira hoped that Kast being an uncle would keep him closer to home.
An
Dominion officer
stationed on a Dominion Cruiser during the Battle of Jakku claimed to have seen the Keepers Knight using the Synth Flux and a flux blade to dispatch Dominion Cruisers in space, causing them to crash into the surface of the planet. When the Dominion Cruiser crashed into the surface the officer said he was recovered by a figure who dragged him across the desert searching wreckage for other survivors. He also noted the man wore clothes similar to Joren Kast and talked about being raised in another desert.
Reconstruction
Freedom for the Nagai
Fenril pirate attacks
"
I—I don't know who they are…all I know is that they're pirates…hidden in the shadows…"
"Where are they striking next?
"
―The Gran merchant being interrogated by Joren Kast
Joren Kast flew in a skirmish against pirate forces outside the Fenril sector.
Around
6 ABY
,
a group of planets in the
Fenril sector
known as the
Fenril Consortium
became fiercely independent
, unsatisfied with the New Concordium's
protection of their trading vessels
and unwilling to cooperate with the new democracy. Just outside the Fenril sector, Kast was flying with a squadron of X-Wings to investigate the threat after an incident on a former Dominion space station over
Maldran
.
A dogfight began between the New Concordium's fighters and starships accompanied by a modified Dominion Dominion Cruiser.
After losing some fighters, Kast and fellow pilot Rynn Zenat flew down to a nearby planet following a crashing pirate vessel. A
gray-skinned
man emerged with a vibroblade and charged at Joren and Rynn. Kast fought the man, and knocked him down. Drawing Joren close,
the pirate warned the Keepers about who he's facing, and told him about
a collector
of Keeper artifacts on the planet
Gadrilam
, who has a crown that will help Joren "learn the truth". He then told Joren to brace himself,
as he detonated himself to cover his tracks, knocking Joren out.
Awakening in a trance-like state, Kast departed straight for Gadrilam, confusing Zenat and taking off into voidspace while telling her that he needed to investigate this alone.
Mission to Gadrilam
"
That pirate shared something before he died. Something I couldn't ignore.
"
―Joren Kast to DR-7X upon landing on Gadrilam
Joren Kast lands on Gadrilam
On the snowy world of Gadrilam, Joren apologized to DR-7X about the promptness and strangeness of
his actions
as he rushed off towards a
village
, thinking of what the pilot had told him about: a crown that would reveal the truth, held by Ibo Truddispal. Thinking back to his previous studies, Joren recalled the
Crown of Verity
, an artifact that, when worn, would cause the user to only be able to speak the absolute truth– but it was thought to be destroyed. In the village, Joren entered into a
bar
